one gets the hadronic tensor (
102
) in the form

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636ptc_{f}^{-2}\check{W}^{\rm ZSa}_{\mu\nu}~=~{N_{c}\over s}\langle A,B|a_{f}^{2}\Psi_{1}\gamma_{\mu}\Psi_{2}(x)\Psi_{2}\gamma_{\nu}\Psi_{1}(0)+\Psi_{1}\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{2}(x)\Psi_{2}\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{1}(0)

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38109pt-~a_{f}\Psi_{1}\gamma_{\mu}\Psi_{2}(x)\Psi_{2}\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{1}(0)-a_{f}\Psi_{1}\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{2}(x)\Psi_{2}\gamma_{\nu}\Psi_{1}(0)|A,B\rangle+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu+x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip 51.21495pt=~(a_{f}^{2}+1)\check{W}_{\mu\nu}^{{\rm F}f}(x)+(a_{f}^{2}-1)\check{W}_{\mu\nu}^{{\rm H}f}(x)-2a_{f}\check{W}_{\mu\nu}^{5f}(x)

(103)

where
W_{\mu\nu}^{{\rm F}f}(x)
and
W_{\mu\nu}^{{\rm H}f}(x)
are defined by Eqs. (
25
) and (
26
) whereas

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{W}_{\mu\nu}^{\rm 5}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over 2s}\big(g_{\mu\nu}g^{\alpha\beta}-\delta_{\mu}^{\alpha}\delta_{\nu}^{\beta}-\delta_{\nu}^{\alpha}\delta_{\mu}^{\beta}\big)\Big\{[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\alpha}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{1}^{n}(0)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}^{n}(0)\gamma^{\beta}\Psi_{2}^{m}(x)]

\displaystyle\hskip 39.83385pt+~\gamma_{\alpha}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{\beta}\leftrightarrow\gamma_{\alpha}\otimes\gamma_{\beta}\gamma_{5}\Big\}+x\leftrightarrow 0.

(104)

for flavor
f
which we are considering. The results for
W_{\mu\nu}^{{\rm F}f}(q)
and
W_{\mu\nu}^{{\rm H}f}(q)
are given by Eqs. (
49
) and (
84
)
from previous Section while
W_{\mu\nu}^{5f}(q)
must be evaluated anew.

We will prove now that

\check{W}_{\mu\nu}^{\rm 5}(x)~=~0

(105)

with our accuracy. It includes the same terms as we assembled to
\check{W}_{\mu\nu}^{{\rm F}}(x)

but with additional
\gamma_{5}
attached to one of the fermion fields. Since extra
\gamma_{5}
cannot change the power
of
s
we need to look how the terms which gave leading contribution to
\check{W}^{{\rm F}}_{\mu\nu}(x)
are affected by extra
\gamma_{5}
.
First, note that the leading-twist first term in the r.h.s. of Eq. (
29
) with extra
\gamma_{5}
can be neglected.
Indeed, if one replaces
\gamma_{\alpha}
by
\gamma_{\alpha}\gamma_{5}

(or
\gamma_{\beta}
by
\gamma_{\beta}\gamma_{5}
) the term
\sim g_{\mu\nu}
vanishes and two other
terms are
\sim{1\over s}p_{2\beta}\epsilon_{\alpha j}q^{j}
as seen from the parametrization (
327
).
Similarly, replacement
\gamma_{\beta}\rightarrow\gamma_{\beta}\gamma_{5}
gives terms of order of
\sim{1\over s}p_{1\alpha}\epsilon_{\beta j}q^{j}

which we neglect, see the discussion after Eq. (
19
).

Next, let us consider sum of terms in
\check{W}^{\rm(1)F}_{1\mu\nu}(x)
and
\check{W}^{\rm(1)F}_{2\mu\nu}(x)
which has the form

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{p_{2\mu}\over s^{2}}\Big[\langle\bar{\psi}(x){\not\!p}_{2}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}{\not\!\!B}(0){\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{\perp}_{\nu}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+~\langle{\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}(x){\not\!p}_{2}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)\gamma_{\nu}^{\perp}{\not\!p}_{1}\bar{\not\!\!B}(x)\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\psi(0)\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\Big]~+~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu~+~x\leftrightarrow 0,

(106)

see Eqs. (
33
) and (
35
).

8
8

8

In these equations we dropped vanishing terms
+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\psi(0)\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)

but now we need them.

It is easy to see that the replacement
\psi(0)\rightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)
gives either
vanishing projectile matrix element or vanishing target matrix element
after using Eqs. (
329
), (
330
), (
336
), and (
337
). Similarly, the sum of contributions
to
\check{W}^{\rm(1)F}_{3\mu\nu}(x)+\check{W}^{\rm(1)F}_{4\mu\nu}(x)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{p_{1\mu}\over s^{2}}\big[\langle\bar{\psi}{\not\!\!A}(x){\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}(0){\not\!p}_{1}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+\langle\bar{\psi}(x)\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}{\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!\!A}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0){\not\!p}_{1}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\psi(0)\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\Big]~+~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(107)

vanishes after replacement
\psi(0)\rightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)
due to QCD equations of motion mentioned above.
Thus,
\check{W}^{(1)5}_{\mu\nu}(x)=0

Let us now consider leading
W^{\rm(2)F}_{\mu\nu}
terms with two gluon operators
discussed in Sect.
4.1.2
, see Eq. (
37
). As we saw,
the leading contribution comes from transverse
\mu
and
\nu
. It is given by sum of Eqs. (
39
), (
40
),
and the corresponding terms coming from

[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\alpha}\gamma_{5}\psi_{A}^{n}(0)][{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\beta}\gamma_{5}\psi_{B}^{m}(x)]

\displaystyle\hskip-31.29802pt\check{W}_{1\mu\nu}^{\rm(2)F}(x)~=~{g_{\mu\nu}^{\parallel}\over s^{3}}\Big(\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x){\not\!\!A}(x){\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0){\not\!\!B}(0){\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt+~\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!\!A}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}{\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!\!B}(x)\psi(x)\rangle_{B}\Big)~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(108)

As was mentioned in the end of Sect.
4.1.2
, from equations (
314
) it is clear that the second term
in the r.h.s. gives the same contribution as the first term. If, however, one replaces
\psi(0)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)

(or, equivalently,
\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(x)
), it is easy to see that the two contributions
cancel so
\check{W}_{\mu\nu}^{\rm(2)F5}(x)=0
.

Next, the leading terms in
\check{W}_{2\mu\nu}^{\rm(2)F}(x)
are given by Eqs. (
44
) and (
46
)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{W}_{2\mu\nu}^{\rm(2)F}(x)~=~-{4p_{1\mu}p_{1\nu}\over s^{4}}\Big(\langle\bar{\psi}(x){\not\!\!A}(x){\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!\!A}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0){\not\!p}_{1}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\Big)-{4p_{2\mu}p_{2\nu}\over s^{4}}\Big(\langle\bar{\psi}(x){\not\!\!B}(x){\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!\!B}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\alpha}\big)(0){\not\!p}_{2}{1\over\alpha}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}+\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\Big)~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(109)

If we now replace
\psi(0)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)
it is easy to see from Eqs. (
330
) and (
345
) that

\check{W}_{\mu\nu}^{(2b)5}(x)=0
. As to
\check{W}_{3\mu\nu}^{\rm(2)F}(x)
, from Eq. (
48
) we see that it is
O\big({1\over N_{c}^{2}}\big)
so we neglect it.

Thus, we obtain the “annihilation part” of symmetric hadronic tensor due to Z-boson currents in the form

\hskip 0.0ptW^{\rm ZSan}_{\mu\nu}~=~e^{2}\sum_{f}c_{f}^{2}\big[(a_{f}^{2}+1)W_{\mu\nu}^{f\rm F}(q)+(a_{f}^{2}-1)W_{\mu\nu}^{f\rm H}(q)\big]

(110)

where
W_{\mu\nu}^{f{\rm F}}(q)
and
W_{\mu\nu}^{f\rm H}(q)
are given by Eqs. (
49
) and (
84
).
Note that it is gauge invariant up to
W_{\mu\nu}^{2{\rm H}}(q)
term discussed in the end of Sect.
4
.

5.1.1
Exchange-type power corrections to
W^{ZS}_{\mu\nu}

Power corrections of the “exchange” type come from the terms

\hskip 0.0pt(\check{W}^{\rm ZS}_{ff^{\prime}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)~=~c_{f}c_{f^{\prime}}\big[a_{f}a_{f^{\prime}}\check{W}^{ff^{\prime}{\rm ex}}_{\mu\nu}(x)+(\check{W}^{{\rm S}ff^{\prime}}_{55})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)-a_{f}(\check{W}^{{\rm S}ff^{\prime}}_{5{\rm a}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)-a_{f^{\prime}}(\check{W}^{{\rm S}ff^{\prime}}_{5{\rm b}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)\big]

(111)

where
\check{\cal W}^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)
is given by Eq. (
90
)
while
(\check{\cal W}^{\rm S}_{55})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)

(\check{\cal W}^{\rm S}_{5A,B})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)
are defined as

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt(\check{W}^{{\rm S}ff^{\prime}}_{55})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)~\equiv~{N_{c}\over s}\langle A,B|[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{1}(x)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{2}(0)]+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle+x\leftrightarrow 0,

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt(\check{W}^{{\rm S}ff^{\prime}}_{5{\rm a}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)~\equiv~{N_{c}\over s}\langle A,B|[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{1}(x)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Psi_{2}(0)]+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle~+~x\leftrightarrow 0,

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt(\check{W}^{{\rm S}ff^{\prime}}_{5{\rm b}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)~\equiv~{N_{c}\over s}\langle A,B|[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Psi_{1}(x)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{2}(0)]+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(112)

As seen from the of comparison parametrizations (
9.4
) and
(
9.4
), the replacement
\psi\rightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi
in the projectile matrix elements
leads to
k_{\mu}j_{1}\rightarrow\pm i\epsilon_{\mu\nu}k^{\nu}j_{1}
,

k_{\mu}j_{2}\rightarrow\pm i\epsilon_{\mu\nu}k^{\nu}j_{2}
. Similarly, the replacement
\psi\rightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi
in target
matrix elements yields
(q-k)_{\mu}j_{1,2}\rightarrow\pm i\epsilon_{\mu}(q-k)^{\nu}j_{1,2}
.
Looking at the result (
99
)
and taking care of signs of replacements
\psi\rightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi
in Eqs. (
9.4
) and (
9.4
), we obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt(\check{W}^{{\rm S}ff^{\prime}}_{55})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)=~{N_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)s^{3}}\Big(\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{\mu}(0)\gamma_{5}\psi(x)+\bar{\psi}(x)\breve{A}_{\mu}(0)\not{p}_{2}{1\over\alpha}\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}^{f}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt\times~\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{\nu}(x)\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}+\bar{\psi}(0)\breve{B}_{\nu}(x)\not{p}_{1}{1\over\beta}\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}^{f^{\prime}}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-{\rm trace}

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt+~g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\breve{A}_{m}(0)\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{5}\psi(x)+\bar{\psi}(x)\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{m}(0){1\over\alpha}\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}^{f}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt\times~\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\breve{B}^{m}(x)\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}+\bar{\psi}(0)\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}^{m}(x){1\over\beta}\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}^{f^{\prime}}\Big)~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\Rightarrow~(W_{\mu\nu}^{{\rm S}ff^{\prime}})_{55}^{\rm ex}(q)~=~-{N_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)Q_{\parallel}^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}\big\{[k^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu+g^{\perp}_{\mu\nu}(k,q-k)_{\perp}]

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt\times~J^{1ff^{\prime}}_{++}(q,k_{\perp})-~g^{\perp}_{\mu\nu}(k,q-k)_{\perp}J^{2ff^{\prime}}_{++}(q,k_{\perp})\big\}

(113)

and

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt(\check{\cal W}^{{\rm S}ff^{\prime}}_{5{\rm a}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)=~{N_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)s^{3}}\Big(\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{\mu}(0)\gamma_{5}\psi(x)+\bar{\psi}(x)\breve{A}_{\mu}(0)\not{p}_{2}{1\over\alpha}\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}^{f}

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt\times~\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{\nu}(x)\psi(0)\rangle_{B}+\bar{\psi}(0)\breve{B}_{\nu}(x)\not{p}_{1}{1\over\beta}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}^{f^{\prime}}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-{\rm trace}

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+~g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\breve{A}_{m}(0)\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{5}\psi(x)+\bar{\psi}(x)\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{m}(0){1\over\alpha}\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}^{f}

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt\times~\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\breve{B}^{m}(x)\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}+\bar{\psi}(0)\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}^{m}(x){1\over\beta}\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}^{f^{\prime}}\Big)~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt\Rightarrow~(W_{\mu\nu}^{{\rm S}ff^{\prime}})_{5{\rm a}}^{\rm ex}(q)~=~-{N_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)Q_{\parallel}^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}[\epsilon_{\mu m}k^{m}(q-k)_{\nu}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu]I^{1ff^{\prime}}_{+-}(q,k_{\perp})

(114)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt(\check{\cal W}^{{\rm S}ff^{\prime}}_{5{\rm b}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)s^{3}}\Big(\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{\mu}(0)\psi(x)+\bar{\psi}(x)\breve{A}_{\mu}(0)\not{p}_{2}{1\over\alpha}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}^{f}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{\nu}(x)\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}+\bar{\psi}(0)\breve{B}_{\nu}(x)\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{5}{1\over\beta}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}^{f^{\prime}}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-{\rm trace}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~g_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\breve{A}_{m}(0)\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{5}\psi(x)+\bar{\psi}(x)\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{m}(0){1\over\alpha}\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}^{f}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\breve{B}^{m}(x)\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}+\bar{\psi}(0)\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}^{m}(x){1\over\beta}\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}^{f^{\prime}}\Big)~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\Rightarrow~(W_{\mu\nu}^{{\rm S}ff^{\prime}})_{5{\rm b}}^{\rm ex}(q)~=~{N_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)Q_{\parallel}^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}[k_{\mu}\epsilon_{\nu n}(q-k)^{n}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu]I^{1ff^{\prime}}_{-+}(q,k_{\perp})

(115)

where

Note that the two last contributions are traceless since

\epsilon_{ij}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}~k^{i}(q-k)^{j}\phi_{1}(k_{\perp}^{2})\phi_{2}((q-k)_{\perp}^{2})~=~0

(116)

for any functions
\phi_{1}
and
\phi_{2}
.

5.1.2
Results for symmetric hadronic tensor for Z-mediated DY process

It is convenient to represent the hadronic tensor
W^{\rm ZS}_{\mu\nu}
as a sum of three parts

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW^{\rm ZS}_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~W^{\rm ZS1}_{\mu\nu}(q)+W^{\rm ZS2}_{\mu\nu}(q)+W^{\rm ZS3}_{\mu\nu}(q)

(117)

The first, gauge-invariant, part is given by Eq. (
103
)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW^{\rm ZS1}_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~e^{2}\sum_{f}c_{f}^{2}\big[(a_{f}^{2}+1)W_{\mu\nu}^{{\rm F}f}(q)+(a_{f}^{2}-1)W_{\mu\nu}^{{\rm H}f}(q)\big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW^{\rm fF}_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~{1\over N_{c}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}F^{f}(q,k_{\perp}){\cal W}^{F}_{\mu\nu}(q,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~W^{\rm fH}_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~{1\over N_{c}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}H^{f}(q,k_{\perp}){\cal W}^{H}_{\mu\nu}(q,k_{\perp})

(118)

where
W_{\mu\nu}^{{\rm F}f}(q)
is given by Eq. (
49
) and
W_{\mu\nu}^{{\rm H}f}(q)

by Eq.

\hskip 0.0ptW^{\rm ZSan}_{\mu\nu}~=~e^{2}\sum_{f}c_{f}^{2}\big[(a_{f}^{2}+1)W_{\mu\nu}^{f\rm F}(q)+(a_{f}^{2}-1)W_{\mu\nu}^{f\rm H}(q)\big]

(119)

where
W_{\mu\nu}^{{\rm F}f}(q)
and
W_{\mu\nu}^{\rm Hf}(q)
are given by Eqs. (
49
) and (
85
).

The second part is

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW^{\rm ZS2}_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~\sum_{f}c_{f}^{2}(a_{f}^{2}-1)W^{\rm 2Hf}_{\mu\nu}(q)

(120)

where
W^{\rm 2Hf}_{\mu\nu}(q)
is given by Eq. (
87
).

The third, “exchange”, part is given by the sum

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW_{\mu\nu}^{\rm ZSex}(q)~=~{N_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)Q_{\parallel}^{2}}\sum_{f,f^{\prime}}c_{f}c_{f^{\prime}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}\Big\{[k^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu+g^{\perp}_{\mu\nu}(k,q-k)_{\perp}]

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt\times~\big(a_{f}a_{f^{\prime}}J^{1ff^{\prime}}_{--}-~J^{1ff^{\prime}}_{++}\big)-~g^{\perp}_{\mu\nu}(k,q-k)_{\perp}\big(a_{f}a_{f^{\prime}}J^{2ff^{\prime}}_{++}-~J^{2ff^{\prime}}_{++}\big)

(121)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~a_{f}[\epsilon_{\mu m}k^{m}(q-k)_{\nu}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu]I^{1ff^{\prime}}_{+-}(q,k_{\perp})-~a_{f^{\prime}}[k_{\mu}\epsilon_{\nu n}(q-k)^{n}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu]I^{1ff^{\prime}}_{-+}(q,k_{\perp})\Big\}

where
J^{i}_{\pm\pm}
are listed in Eq. (
450
) and
I^{i}_{\pm\pm}
in Eq. (
451
).

As in the photon case, the exchange power corrections are non-zero only for transverse
\mu
and
\nu

in our approximation.

5.2
Antisymmetric part of
Z
-boson hadronic tensor

The antisymmetric part of hadronic tensor for cross section mediated by Z-boson is defined in Eq. (
10
) where we should make substitution
\psi\rightarrow\Psi_{1}+\Psi_{2}
. Let us start from annihilation-type contribution

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{W}^{\rm ZAa}_{\mu\nu}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over s}\langle A,B|{\cal J}_{12\mu}(x){\cal J}_{21\nu}(0)-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle-x\leftrightarrow 0

(122)

Using Fierz transformations (
285
) and (
288
) it can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{W}^{\rm ZAa}_{\mu\nu}~=~\sum_{f}c_{f}^{2}\Big[{i\over 2}\epsilon_{\mu\nu}^{~~\alpha\beta}\big[-2a_{f}\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\alpha\beta}^{f\cal F}+(a_{f}^{2}+1)\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\alpha\beta}^{f5}\big]+(a_{f}^{2}-1)\check{W}^{\rm as}_{\mu\nu}\Big]

(123)

where

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{\cal F}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over 2s}\langle A,B|[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Psi_{1}^{n}(0)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Psi_{2}^{m}(x)]

(124)

\displaystyle\hskip 39.83385pt+~\gamma_{\mu}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\leftrightarrow\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle-x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{\rm 5}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over 2s}\langle A,B|[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{1}^{n}(0)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Psi_{2}^{m}(x)]

\displaystyle\hskip 39.83385pt+~\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\leftrightarrow\gamma_{\mu}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle-x\leftrightarrow 0,

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{W}^{\rm as}_{\mu\nu}(x)~=~{iN_{c}\over 2s}\langle A,B|-[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\Psi_{1}^{n}(0)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}^{n}(0)\sigma_{\mu\nu}\Psi_{2}^{m}(x)]

\displaystyle\hskip 39.83385pt+~[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\sigma_{\mu\nu}\Psi_{1}^{n}(0)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}^{n}(0)\Psi_{2}^{m}(x)]+[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\gamma_{5}\Psi_{1}^{n}(0)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}^{n}(0)\sigma_{\mu\nu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{2}^{m}(x)]

\displaystyle\hskip 39.83385pt-[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\sigma_{\mu\nu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{1}^{n}(0)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}^{n}(0)\gamma_{5}\Psi_{2}^{m}(x)]\big\}|A,B\rangle-x\leftrightarrow 0

for the flavor under consideration.

Let us start from the
\mathpzc{W}_{\mu\nu}^{\cal F}(x)
given by the first line in Eq. (
124
) and compare it to

W_{\mu\nu}(x)
for the photon case. It is easy to see that if we take
W^{\rm F}_{\mu\nu}(x)
and and antisymmetrize with respect to
\mu
and
\nu
instead of symmetrization, we will get
\mathpzc{W}_{\mu\nu}^{\cal F}(x)
.
Since antisymmetrization
vs
symmetrization
does not affect power counting in
{q_{\perp}^{2}\over s}
parameter (or
\alpha_{q},\beta_{q}\ll 1
parameter), we can consider only terms
that gave leading contribution to
W_{\mu\nu}(x)
.

First, consider the leading-twist contribution

9
9

9

Recall that “check” means
W
’s in coordinate space multiplied by
{2N_{c}\over s}
, cf. Eq. (
20
)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{{\cal F},{\rm lt}}(x)~=~{1\over 2s}\big(\langle\bar{\psi}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\gamma_{\mu}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}_{B}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi(x_{\star},x_{\perp})\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt+~\gamma_{\mu}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\leftrightarrow\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\big)~-~x\leftrightarrow 0.

(125)

Using parametrizations (
322
) and (
326
) we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\mathpzc{W}_{\mu\nu}^{{\cal F},{\rm lt}}(q)~=~{1\over 16\pi^{4}}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\star}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}-i\beta_{q}x_{\star}+i(q,x)_{\perp}}\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{{\cal F},lt}(x)

\displaystyle\hskip 45.5244pt=~-{2\over s}\big(p_{1\mu}p_{2\nu}-p_{1\nu}p_{2\mu}\big)\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}~\mathpzc{F}(q,k_{\perp})

(126)

where

\mathpzc{F}^{f}(q,k_{\perp})~=~f_{1}^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp}){\bar{f}}_{1}^{f}(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})~-~f_{1}^{f}\leftrightarrow{\bar{f}}_{1}^{f}

(127)

As usually, term with
f_{1}\leftrightarrow{\bar{f}}_{1}
comes from
x\leftrightarrow 0
contribution.

Next, we consider terms with gluon operators and separate them as in Sect.
4.1
according to number of gluon fields (contained in
\Xi
’s ):

\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{\cal F}(x)~{=}~\check{\cal W}_{\mu\nu}^{{\cal F},lt}+\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{1{\cal F}}(x)+\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{(2a){\cal F}}+\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{(2b){\cal F}}+\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{(2c){\cal F}}

(128)

where leading-twist term
\check{\cal W}_{\mu\nu}^{{\cal F},lt}
was considered above, and

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{1{\cal F}}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over 2s}\langle A,B|\big[\bar{\psi}_{A}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{1}^{n}(0)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{B}^{m}(x)\big]

(129)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}^{n}(0)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{A}^{m}(x)\big]+\big[\bar{\psi}_{A}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}^{n}(0)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x)\big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\big[\bar{\psi}_{A}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}^{n}(0)\big]\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{B}^{m}(x)\big]+~\gamma_{\mu}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\leftrightarrow\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{(2a){\cal F}}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over 2s}\langle A,B|\big[\bar{\psi}_{A}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{1}^{n}(0)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x)\big]

(130)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}^{n}(0)\big]\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{B}^{m}(x)\big]+~\gamma_{\mu}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\leftrightarrow\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{(2b){\cal F}}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over 2s}\langle A,B|\big[\bar{\psi}_{A}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}^{n}(0)\big]\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x)\big]

(131)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{1}^{n}(0)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{B}^{m}(x)\big]+~\gamma_{\mu}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\leftrightarrow\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

and

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{(2c){\cal F}}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over 2s}\langle A,B|\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}^{n}(0)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x)\big]

(132)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\big[\bar{\psi}_{A}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{1}^{n}(0)\big]\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{B}^{m}(x)\big]+~\gamma_{\mu}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\leftrightarrow\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

The corresponding contributions to
\mathpzc{W}_{\mu\nu}(q)
will be denoted
\mathpzc{W}_{\mu\nu}^{(1){\cal F}}
,
\mathpzc{W}_{\mu\nu}^{(2a){\cal F}}
,
\mathpzc{W}_{\mu\nu}^{(2b){\cal F}}
, and

\mathpzc{W}_{\mu\nu}^{(2c){\cal F}}
, respectively.
We will consider these contributions in turn following the analysis in Sect.
4.1
.

5.2.1
One-gluon terms in
\mathpzc{W}_{\mu\nu}^{\cal F}

First, we consider terms with one gluon operator and start with

\big[\bar{\psi}_{A}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{1}^{n}(0)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{B}^{m}(x)\big]
.
Using
\Xi_{1}~=~-{\not{p}_{2}\over s}\gamma^{i}B_{i}{1\over\alpha}\psi_{A}
and separating color-singlet terms, we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{1\mu\nu}^{(1){\cal F}}(x)~=~-{1\over 2s^{2}}\big\{\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x)\gamma_{\mu}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{i}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt~+~(\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)~-~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\big\}~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

(133)

As we discussed above, we need to consider only terms which gave leading contribution for symmetric case,
i.e. with one index longitudinal and the other transverse. Similarly to Eq. (
33
), for longitudinal
\mu

and transverse
\nu
we get

\displaystyle\hskip-31.29802pt\Big({2p_{1}^{\mu}p_{2}^{\mu^{\prime}}\over s}+\mu\leftrightarrow\mu^{\prime}\Big)\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{(1){\cal F}}(x)~

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-\Big({p_{2\mu}p_{1}^{\mu^{\prime}}\over s^{3}}+\mu\leftrightarrow\mu^{\prime}\Big)\big\{\langle\bar{\psi}(x)\gamma_{\mu^{\prime}}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}B^{i}(0)\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt+~(\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)~-~\mu^{\prime}\leftrightarrow\nu\big\}~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

(134)

The term proportional to
p_{2\mu}
in the r.h.s. can be expressed using Eq. (
303
) as follows:

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{p_{2\mu}\over s^{3}}\Big[\langle\bar{\psi}(x)\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}B^{i}(0){\not\!p}_{1}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-\langle\bar{\psi}(x){\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}B^{i}(0)\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt+~(\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\Big]~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{p_{2\mu}\over s^{3}}\Big[\langle\bar{\psi}(x){\not\!p}_{2}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}(0){\not\!\!B}(0){\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt+~(\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x))\Big]~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

(135)

since the second term in the first line is
O({q_{\perp}^{2}\over s}\big)
with respect to the first one.
Next, if
\nu
is longitudinal and
\mu
transverse, we consider
\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{1\nu\mu}^{(1)F}=-\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{1\mu\nu}^{(1)F}
, repeat the above calculation and get result (
135
) with
\mu\leftrightarrow\nu
. Thus, the case with longitudinal
\nu
and transverse
\mu
is obtained from (
135
) by

-(\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)
replacement, so
using Eqs. (
329
), (
334
) and parametrizations from Sect.
9.2
we obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\mathpzc{W}_{1\mu\nu}^{(1){\cal F}}(q)~=~{p_{2\mu}\over\alpha_{q}s}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}~(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}\mathpzc{F}(q,k_{\perp})~-~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu

(136)

which is the same as Eq. (
34
), only with antisymmetrization in
\mu,\nu
and
f\leftrightarrow{\bar{f}}
instead of symmetrizaton.

The second term in the r.h.s. of Eq. (
129
) with longitudinal
\mu
and transverse
\nu
can be obtained in a similar way.
Repeating steps from Eq. (
133
) to Eq. (
135
), we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{2\mu\nu}^{(1){\cal F}}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over 2s}\langle A,B|\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}^{n}(0)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{A}^{m}(x)\big]|A,B\rangle

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt+~\gamma_{\mu}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\leftrightarrow\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-{1\over 2s^{2}}\big\{\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{\mu}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)\gamma_{\nu}B^{i}(x)\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt+~(\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)~-~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\big\}~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-\Big({p_{2\mu}p_{1}^{\mu^{\prime}}\over s^{3}}+\mu\leftrightarrow\mu^{\prime}\Big)\big\{\langle\bar{\psi}(x)\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{\mu^{\prime}}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}(0)\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}B^{i}(x)\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt+~(\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)~-~\mu^{\prime}\leftrightarrow\nu\big\}~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\simeq~{p_{2\mu}\over s^{3}}\Big[\langle\bar{\psi}(x){\not\!p}_{2}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}(0)\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}{\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!\!B}(x)\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt+~(\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x))\Big]~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

(137)

The opposite case with transverse
\mu
and longitudinal
\nu
is obtained by
-(\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)

and therefore from Eq. (
337
) we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\mathpzc{W}_{2\mu\nu}^{(1){\cal F}}(q)~=~{p_{2\mu}\over\alpha_{q}s}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}~(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}\mathpzc{F}(q,k_{\perp})~-~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu

(138)

which doubles the result (
136
) similarly to the symmetric case.

Rewriting now the third term in the r.h.s. of Eq. (
129
) with longitudinal
\mu
and transverse
\nu
and repeating the above steps,
we get (recall
\Xi_{2}~=~-{\not{p}_{1}\over s}\gamma^{i}A_{i}{1\over\beta}\psi_{B}
):

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{1\mu\nu}^{(2){\cal F}}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over 2s}\langle A,B|\big[\bar{\psi}_{A}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}^{n}(0)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x)\big]

(139)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\gamma_{\mu}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\leftrightarrow\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-{1\over 2s^{2}}\big\{\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A^{i}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)\gamma_{\nu}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt+~(\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)~-~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\big\}~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-\Big({p_{1\mu}p_{2}^{\mu^{\prime}}\over s^{3}}+\mu\leftrightarrow\mu^{\prime}\Big)\big\{\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A^{i}(x)\gamma_{\mu^{\prime}}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)\gamma_{\nu}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt+~(\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)~-~\mu^{\prime}\leftrightarrow\nu\big\}~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{p_{1\mu}\over s^{3}}\big\{\langle\bar{\psi}(x){\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{\nu_{\perp}}(x)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0){\not\!p}_{1}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt+~(\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\big\}~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

As above, the case with longitudinal
\nu
and transverse
\mu
is obtained from (
139
) by

-(\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)
replacement, so
using Eqs. (
329
), (
334
) and parametrizations from Sect.
9.2
we obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\mathpzc{W}_{1\mu\nu}^{(2){\cal F}}(q)~=~-{p_{1\mu}\over\beta_{q}s}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}~k^{\perp}_{\nu}\mathpzc{F}(q,k_{\perp})~-~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu

(140)

Finally, similarly to Eq. (
137
), it can be demonstrated that the forth term in the r.h.s. of Eq. (
129
)
\sim\big[\bar{\psi}_{A}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}^{n}(0)\big]\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{B}^{m}(x)\big]
doubles the contribution (
140
) of the third term,
so we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\mathpzc{W}_{\mu\nu}^{(1){\cal F}}(q)~=~2\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}~\Big[{p_{2\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}\over\alpha_{q}s}-{p_{1\mu}k^{\perp}_{\nu}\over\beta_{q}s}\Big]\mathpzc{F}(q,k_{\perp})~-~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu

(141)

Note that it can be obtained from Eq. (
36
) by replacement of symmetrization in
\mu\leftrightarrow\nu
and

f\leftrightarrow{\bar{f}}
with antisymmetrization.

5.2.2
Two-gluon terms in
\mathpzc{W}_{\mu\nu}^{\cal F}

We start from the first term in the r.h.s. of Eq. (
130
)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{1\mu\nu}^{(2a){\cal F}}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over 2s}\langle A,B|\big[\bar{\psi}_{A}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{1}^{n}(0)\big]\big[\bar{\psi}_{B}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x)\big]

(142)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\gamma_{\mu}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\leftrightarrow\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

Separating color-singlet contributions one can rewrite Eq. (
142
) as

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{1\mu\nu}^{(2a){\cal F}}(x)~=~{1\over 2s^{3}}\big\{\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{i}(x)\gamma_{\mu}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{j}(0)\gamma_{\nu}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\}~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

(143)

Similar to the symmetric case discussed in Sect.
4.1.2
, the leading term comes from
\mu
and
\nu
that are both transverse.
In this case we can use formula (
311
) and get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{1\mu_{\perp}\nu_{\perp}}^{(2a){\cal F}}(x)~=~{1\over 2s^{3}}\big\{\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{i}(x)\gamma_{\mu_{\perp}}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{j}(0)\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

(144)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\}~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=~-{1\over s^{3}}\Big(\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x){\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{\mu}(x){1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0){\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}_{\nu}(0){1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\Big)~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

Using now Eqs. (
329
), (
334
) and (
336
) we obtain contribution to
\mathpzc{W}_{\mu\nu}^{F}(q)

in the form

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\mathpzc{W}_{1\mu_{\perp}\nu_{\perp}}^{(2a){\cal F}}(x)=~{1\over 16\pi^{4}}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\star}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}-i\beta_{q}x_{\star}+i(q,x)_{\perp}}\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{1\mu_{\perp}\nu_{\perp}}^{(2a)F}(x)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{1\over Q_{\parallel}^{2}}\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}[k^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu]\mathpzc{F}(q,k_{\perp})~=~0.

(145)

This term vanishes after integration over
k_{\perp}
but we will keep it for a while since we want to have
gauge invariance at the integrand level, see Eq. (
151
) below.

Next, second term in the r.h.s. of Eq. (
130
)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{2\mu\nu}^{(2a){\cal F}}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over 2s}\langle A,B|[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}^{n}(0)\big]\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{B}^{m}(x)\big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\gamma_{\mu}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\leftrightarrow\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

(146)

at transverse
\mu
and
\nu
can be transformed to

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{2\mu_{\perp}\nu_{\perp}}^{(2a){\cal F}}(x)~=~{1\over 2s^{3}}\big\{\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\gamma^{j}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{\mu_{\perp}}A_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}B_{j}(x)\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

(147)

\displaystyle\hskip 25.60747pt+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\}~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip 25.60747pt=~-{1\over s^{3}}\big\{\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\breve{A}_{\mu_{\perp}}(0){\not\!p}_{2}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\breve{B}_{\nu_{\perp}}(x){\not\!p}_{1}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\}~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

where we used formula (
311
). It is easy to see that the corresponding contribution to

\mathpzc{W}_{2\mu_{\perp}\nu_{\perp}}^{(2a){\cal F}}(q)
doubles the result (
145
), same as for Eq. (
41
) in the symmetric case,
and we obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\mathpzc{W}_{\mu_{\perp}\nu_{\perp}}^{(2a){\cal F}}(x)~=~{2\over Q_{\parallel}^{2}}\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}[k^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu]\mathpzc{F}(q,k_{\perp}).

(148)

Again, this integral vanishes, but we keep the integrand as a part of Eq. (
151
) in order to
have gauge invariance (
152
) visible at the integrand level.

As one can anticipate from Eq. (
45
) for the symmetric case, the contribution from Eq. (
131
)
vanishes. Indeed, e.g. for the first term we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{N_{c}\over 2s}\langle A,B|\big[\bar{\psi}_{A}^{m}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}^{n}(0)\big]\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}^{n}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Xi_{2}^{m}(x)\big]+~\gamma_{\mu}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\leftrightarrow\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{p_{1\nu}\over s^{3}}\Big(\langle\bar{\psi}(x)A_{j}(x)\gamma_{\mu}A_{i}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\Big)~-~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu

(149)

Next,
if the index
\mu
is transverse, the contribution of this equation to
\mathpzc{W}^{{\cal F}}_{\mu\nu}
is of order of
p_{1\nu}q^{\perp}_{\mu}{m_{\perp}^{2}\over\beta_{q}^{2}s^{2}}

(cf. Eq. (
43
)) which is
O\big({q_{\perp}^{2}\over\beta_{q}s}\big)
in comparison to Eq. (
141
). Also, if index
\nu
is longitudinal, the contribution is

\sim p_{1\nu}p_{2\mu}{q_{\perp}^{4}\over\beta_{q}^{2}s^{3}}
which is
O\big({\alpha_{q}^{2}m^{4}\over Q_{\parallel}^{4}}\big)
in comparison
to leading-twist result (
126
).
Thus,

\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{(2b){\cal F}}(x)~=~0
with our accuracy.
Finally, as discussed in Sect.
4.1.2
, the term
\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{(2c){\cal F}}(x)
is of order of
{1\over N_{c}^{2}}
and can be neglected.

5.2.3
Sum of
\mathpzc{W}_{\mu\nu}^{{\cal F}}
terms

Adding the contributions (
126
), (
141
), and (
148
) we obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\mathpzc{W}_{\mu\nu}^{{\cal F}}(q)~=~\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}~\mathpzc{F}(q,k_{\perp})\mathpzc{W}^{F}_{\mu\nu}(q,k_{\perp})

(150)

where
\mathpzc{F}^{f}(q,k_{\perp})
is given by Eq. (
127
) and

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\mathpzc{W}^{F}_{\mu\nu}(q,k_{\perp})~=~{2p_{1\nu}p_{2\mu}\over s}+{2p_{2\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}\over\alpha_{q}s}+{2p_{1\nu}k^{\perp}_{\mu}\over\beta_{q}s}+{2k^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}s}~-~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{q_{\mu}\over Q_{\parallel}^{2}}({\tilde{q}}_{\nu}+q^{\perp}_{\nu}-2k^{\perp}_{\nu})~-~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu

(151)

The corresponding contribution to antisymmetric part of
Z
-boson hadronic tensor
\check{\cal W}^{\rm asy}_{\mu\nu}(q)
is proportional to

\epsilon_{\mu\nu\lambda\rho}\mathpzc{W}^{\lambda\rho}(q)
so we immediately see gauge invariance:

q_{\mu}\epsilon^{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}\mathpzc{W}^{F}_{\alpha\beta}(q,k_{\perp})~=~0

(152)

5.2.4
\mathpzc{W}_{\mu\nu}^{\rm f5}
contribution

From Eq. (
124
) we see that
\mathpzc{W}_{\mu\nu}^{\rm{\cal F}}
and
\mathpzc{W}_{\mu\nu}^{\rm 5}
differ by replacement
\psi(0)\rightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)
.
Let us consider terms assembled in
\mathpzc{W}_{\mu\nu}^{{\cal F}}
and prove that they vanish after such replacement.
Fist, for the leading-twist contribution it is evident from parametrizations (
327
).
Second, let us write down

\displaystyle\hskip-31.29802pt\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{1\mu\nu}^{(1){\cal F}}(x)+\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{2\mu\nu}^{(1){\cal F}}(x)~=~

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt=~{p_{1\mu}\over s^{3}}\big\{\langle\bar{\psi}(x){\not\!p}_{2}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}(0){\not\!\!B}(0){\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+\langle\bar{\psi}(x){\not\!p}_{2}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\langle\bar{\psi}(0)\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}{\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!\!B}(x)\psi(x)\rangle_{B}+~(\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\big\}~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

(153)

The contributions of these two terms are equal, but using equations from Sect.
9.3

it is easy to see that after replacement
\psi(0)\rightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)

they cancel each other as in the Eq. (
154
) case so
\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{1\mu\nu}^{(1)5}(x)=0
. Similarly,
one can demonstrate that

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{W}^{\rm(1)F5}_{3\mu\nu}(x)+\check{W}^{\rm(1)F5}_{4\mu\nu}(x)~=~{p_{1\mu}\over s^{2}}\Big[\langle\bar{\psi}{\not\!\!A}(x){\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\bar{\psi}(0){\not\!p}_{1}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+~\langle\bar{\psi}(x)\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}{\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!\!A}(0)\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0){\not\!p}_{1}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt+~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\psi(0)\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\Big]~+~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu~+~x\leftrightarrow 0

(154)

vanishes since the two terms in the r.h.s. cancel each other.

Let us turn now to terms with two gluon operators and start from
\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{\rm(2a)F5}(x)
. The

\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{(2a){\cal F}}(x)
contribution is given by sum of Eqs. (
144
) and (
147
)

\displaystyle\hskip-31.29802pt\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{(2a){\cal F}}(x)~=~-{1\over s^{3}}\Big(\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x){\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{\mu}(x){1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0){\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}_{\nu}(0){1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt+~\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\breve{A}_{\mu_{\perp}}(0){\not\!p}_{2}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\breve{B}_{\nu_{\perp}}(x){\not\!p}_{1}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}\Big)~-~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

(155)

As we saw in Sect.
5.2.2
, the contributions of the two terms in the r.h.s. are equal. Now, when we replace
\psi(0)\rightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)
,
target matrix elements terms remain the same and projectile ones become of different sign as seen from Eq. (
343
) so

\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{\rm(2a)F5}(x)=0

Finally, the contribution
\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{\rm(2b)F5}(x)
is small by power counting (see Eq. (
149
) and subsequent discussion)
while
\check{\mathpzc{W}}_{\mu\nu}^{\rm(2c)5}(x)
has extra
{1\over N_{c}^{2}}
so we neglect it.
Thus,
\mathpzc{W}_{\mu\nu}^{5}=0
with our accuracy.

As to
W^{\rm as}_{\mu\nu}
defined in Eq. (
124
), it also vanishes with our accuracy but the calculation is more tedious.

5.3
W^{\rm as}_{\mu\nu}

In this Section we will prove that
W^{\rm as}_{\mu\nu}
defined in Eq. (
124
) is small in our approximation. As usual, for power counting we consider

W^{\rm as}_{\mu\nu}(x)
multiplied by
2N_{c}/s
:

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{W}^{\rm as}_{\mu\nu}(x)~=~{iN_{c}\over 2s}\big\{[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\sigma_{\mu\nu}\Psi_{1}^{n}(0)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}^{n}(0)\Psi_{2}^{m}(x)]-[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}^{m}(x)\Psi_{1}^{n}(0)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}^{n}(0)\sigma_{\mu\nu}\Psi_{2}^{m}(x)]

\displaystyle\hskip 36.98857pt-~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\big\}-x\leftrightarrow 0

(156)

First, from parametrizations in Sect
9.2
it is clear that the leading-twist contribution to
\check{W}^{\rm as}_{\mu\nu}
is of order of

{p_{1\mu}q^{\perp}_{\nu}\over s}
(or
{p_{2\mu}q^{\perp}_{\nu}\over s}
) which is
O(\beta_{q})
(or
O(\alpha_{q})
) with respect to contribution (
151
).

5.3.1
One-gluon terms

Next, consider terms with one gluon field and start from term with
\Xi_{1}~=~-{\not{p}_{2}\over s}\gamma^{i}B_{i}{1\over\alpha}\psi_{A}
. We get the contribution
to the r.h.s. of Eq. (
156
) in the form

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{i\over s^{2}}\big\{\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x){\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)B^{i}(0)\sigma_{\mu\nu}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

(157)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x)\sigma_{\mu\nu}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)B_{i}(0)\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\big\}-x\leftrightarrow 0

Let us first take transverse
\mu
and
\nu
and consider the first term in the r.h.s.
Since the projectile matrix element
\langle\psi(x){\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}
is proportional to
x_{i}
and the target one

\langle\psi(0)B^{i}(0)\sigma^{\perp}_{\mu\nu}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}
to
\delta_{\mu}^{i}x^{\perp}_{\nu}-\delta_{\nu}^{i}x^{\perp}_{\mu}
, this term vanishes.
Since
\sigma^{\perp}_{\mu\nu}\gamma^{i}=i(\delta_{\nu}^{i}\gamma^{\perp}_{\mu}-\delta_{\mu}^{i}\gamma^{\perp}_{\nu})
, the second term in the r.h.s. of Eq. (
157
)
vanishes for the same reason: target matrix element is proportional to
x_{i}
and the projectile one to
\delta_{\mu}^{i}x^{\perp}_{\nu}-\delta_{\nu}^{i}x^{\perp}_{\mu}
.
Next, consider terms with extra
\gamma_{5}
’s. Since
\langle\psi(x){\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}\gamma_{5}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\sim\epsilon_{ij}x^{j}
and

\langle\psi(0)B^{i}(0)\sigma^{\perp}_{\mu\nu}\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}={2i\over s}\epsilon^{\perp}_{\mu\nu}\langle\psi(0)B^{i}(0)\sigma^{\perp}_{{\bullet}\star}\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}\sim x^{i}
, this term also gives no contribution. For the last term, since
\sigma_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}\gamma_{5}=i\epsilon_{\mu\nu}^{\perp}{2\over s}\sigma_{\star{\bullet}}
, the projectile matrix elements proportional to
x^{i}
and the target one to
\epsilon_{ij}x^{j}
so the last term in the r.h.s. of Eq. (
157
) vanishes.
Now, terms with
{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}~=~-\big(\bar{\psi}_{A}{1\over\alpha}\big)\gamma^{i}B_{i}{\not{p}_{2}\over s}
are
similar to that of Eq. (
157
) so they vanish for the same reason. Finally, the results for
{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}
and
\Xi_{2}
differ by usual projectile
\leftrightarrow
target
replacements so we get the result that one-gluon contributions to
\check{W}^{\rm as}_{\mu\nu}
vanish at transverse
\mu
and
\nu
.

If now both
\mu
and
\nu
are longitudinal,
\sigma_{\mu\nu}={4\over s^{2}}(p_{1\mu}p_{2\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)\sigma_{\star{\bullet}}
. It is easy to see that

\sigma_{\star{\bullet}}
in the target matrix element brings no factor of
s
while in the projectile one
\sigma_{\star{\bullet}}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}=s\sigma_{{\bullet}i}

can bring
s^{2}
. However, even in this case the corresponding contribution to r.h.s. of Eq. (
157
) is proportional to

{2\over\alpha_{q}s^{2}}(p_{1\mu}p_{2\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)={\beta_{q}\over q_{\parallel}^{2}}\times{2\over s}(p_{1\mu}p_{2\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)
which is

O(\beta_{q})
smaller than the last term in Eq. (
151
)

\sim\epsilon_{\mu\nu ij}{k^{i}(q-k)^{j}\over q_{\parallel}^{2}}\sim{2\over s}(p_{1\mu}p_{2\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu){q_{\perp}^{2}\over q^{2}}
.

Finally, let us consider case when one of the indices is longitudinal and the other transverse. The corresponding contribution
to
\check{W}^{\rm as}_{\mu\nu}(x)
is (cf. Eq. (
62
))

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{2p_{2\mu}\over s^{3}}\big\{\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x)\sigma_{\star i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)B^{i}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-~\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x)\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}\sigma_{\star i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)B^{i}(0)\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\big\}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-x\leftrightarrow 0

(158)

Using formulas (
292
) it is easy to see that the second (and the fourth) term can be neglected since the corresponding contribution
to
\check{W}^{\rm as}_{\mu\nu}(q)
is
\sim(p_{2\mu}q^{\perp}_{\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu){q_{\perp}^{2}\over\alpha_{q}s^{2}}
which is
O(\alpha_{q})
in comparison
to the second term in the r.h.s. of Eq. (
151
)
\sim\epsilon_{\nu j}{p_{2\mu}q^{j}\over\alpha_{q}s}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu
so we are left with

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{2p_{2\mu}\over s^{3}}\big\{\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x)\sigma_{\star i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)B^{i}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt-~\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x)\sigma_{\star i}\gamma_{5}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)B^{i}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}\big\}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-x\leftrightarrow 0

(159)

Next, from Eq. (
295
) we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\sigma_{\star i}\otimes\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}-\sigma_{\star i}\gamma_{5}\otimes\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}\gamma_{5}

(160)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-g_{i\nu}\sigma_{\star l}\otimes\sigma_{\bullet}^{~l}+\sigma_{\star\nu_{\perp}}\otimes\sigma_{{\bullet}i}+\sigma_{\star i}\otimes\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}-{s\over 4}g_{i\nu}\sigma_{mn}\otimes\sigma^{mn}+{s\over 2}\sigma_{\nu_{\perp}l}\otimes\sigma_{i}^{~l}

The two last terms can bring only factor
s
to Eq. (
159
) while the first three bring
\sim s^{2}
so we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{2p_{2\mu}\over s^{3}}\big\{\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x)\sigma_{\star i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)\big[B^{i}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}-B_{\nu}(0)\sigma_{\bullet}^{~i}\big]\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt+~\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x)\sigma_{\star\nu_{\perp}}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)B^{i}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}i}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}\big\}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt=-~i\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x)\sigma_{\star\nu_{\perp}}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0){\not\!\!B}(0){\not\!p}_{1}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-x\leftrightarrow 0

(161)

where we used Eq. (
53
).

Let us calculate now the corresponding contribution to r.h.s. of Eq. (
156
) coming from
{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}(x)
(see Eq. (
16
)):

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt{i\over s^{2}}\big\{\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}(x){1\over\alpha}\big)\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{2}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)B^{i}(0)\sigma_{\mu\nu}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

(162)

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt-~\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\sigma_{\mu\nu}\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{2}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)B_{i}(0)\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\big\}-x\leftrightarrow 0

The only difference from Eq. (
156
) is the sign
\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{2}=-{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{i}
, replacement
B^{i}(0)\rightarrow B^{i}(x)
and
{1\over\alpha}
acting on

{\bar{\psi}}
instead of
\psi
so we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-{2p_{2\mu}\over s^{3}}\big\{\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)\big[B^{i}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}-B_{\nu}(0)\sigma_{\bullet}^{~i}\big]\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt+~\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\sigma_{\star\nu_{\perp}}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)B^{i}(x)\sigma_{{\bullet}i}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}\big\}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt=~-i\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\sigma_{\star\nu_{\perp}}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0){\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!\!B}(x)\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-x\leftrightarrow 0

(163)

and the sum of these contributions to
\check{W}^{\rm as}_{\mu\nu}(x)
takes the form

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-i\big(\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x)\sigma_{\star\nu_{\perp}}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0){\not\!\!B}(0){\not\!p}_{1}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt+~\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\sigma_{\star\nu_{\perp}}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0){\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!\!B}(x)\psi(x)\rangle_{B}\big)-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-x\leftrightarrow 0

(164)

Now, from Eqs. (
329
), (
330
), and (
342
) it is easy to see that the corresponding contribution to

\check{W}^{\rm as}_{\mu\nu}(q)
vanishes due to cancellation between the two terms in the above equation.
Similarly, one can demonstrate that one-gluon contributions to
\check{W}^{\rm as}_{\mu\nu}(q)
from

\Xi_{2}~=~-{\not{p}_{1}\over s}\gamma^{i}A_{i}{1\over\beta+i\epsilon}\psi_{B}
and

{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}~=~-\big(\bar{\psi}_{B}{1\over\beta-i\epsilon}\big)\gamma^{i}A_{i}{\not{p}_{1}\over s}

cancel.

5.3.2
Two-gluon terms

Following analysis in Sect.
4.1.2
, let us start with the contribution to the r.h.s. of Eq. (
156
)
coming from
\Xi_{1}
and
\Xi_{2}
(see Eq. (
16
)).

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{i\over 2s^{3}}\big\{\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{j}(x)\sigma_{\star i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{i}(0)\sigma_{\mu\nu}\sigma_{{\bullet}j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}~-~\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{j}(x)\sigma_{\mu\nu}\sigma_{\star i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt\times~\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{i}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\big\}-x\leftrightarrow 0

(165)

The r.h.s. of Eq. (
165
) at transverse
\mu
and
\nu
due to Eq. (
292
) can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt{\rm Eq.~(\ref{chew9})}~=~{1\over 2s^{3}}\big\{\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{\mu}(x)\sigma_{\star i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{i}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-~\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\sigma_{\star\nu_{\perp}}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt\times~\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{\mu}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\big\}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-x\leftrightarrow 0.

(166)

Using now Eq. (
318
) we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{\rm Eq.~(\ref{chew9})}~=~{1\over 2s^{3}}\big\{\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{\mu}(x)\sigma_{\star i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}\big[B^{i}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}-B_{\nu}(0)\sigma_{\bullet}^{~i}\big]{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt+~\langle{\bar{\psi}}A_{\mu}(x)\sigma_{\star\nu_{\perp}}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{i}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\langle{\bar{\psi}}\big[A_{\nu}(x)\sigma_{\star i}-A^{i}(x)\sigma_{\star\nu_{\perp}}\big]{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{\mu}(0)\sigma_{\bullet}^{~i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt-~\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\sigma_{\star i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B_{\mu}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}\big\}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{1\over 2s^{3}}\big\{\langle{\bar{\psi}}[A_{\mu}(x)\sigma_{\star\nu_{\perp}}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu]{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{i}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\sigma_{\star i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}[B_{\mu}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu]{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}\big\}-x\leftrightarrow 0~=~0

(167)

where we used Eq. (
53
). Next, the
contribution to the r.h.s. of Eq. (
156
) coming from
{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}
and
{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}
has the form

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{i\over 2s^{3}}\big\{\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\sigma_{\star i}A^{j}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}j}\sigma_{\mu\nu}B^{i}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}~-~\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\sigma_{\star i}\sigma_{\mu\nu}A^{j}(0)\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt\times~\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}j}B^{i}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\big\}-x\leftrightarrow 0

(168)

and vanishes for transverse
\mu
and
\nu
for the same reason as the r.h.s. of Eq. (
167
).

If both
\mu
and
\nu
are longitudinal, we get contribution to
\check{W}^{\rm as}_{\mu\nu}(x)
in the form

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{\rm Eq.~(\ref{chew9})}~=~{2i\over s^{5}}(p_{1\mu}p_{2\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)\big\{\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{j}(x)\sigma_{\star i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{i}(0)\sigma_{\star{\bullet}}\sigma_{{\bullet}j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{j}(x)\sigma_{\star{\bullet}}\sigma_{\star i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{i}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\big\}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~x\leftrightarrow 0~=~{2\over s^{4}}(p_{1\mu}p_{2\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)\big\{\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{j}(x)\sigma_{\star i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{i}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt-~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\big\}-x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{2\over s^{4}}(p_{1\mu}p_{2\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)\big\{\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\sigma_{\star i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{j}(x)\sigma_{\star i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}[B^{i}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}j}-B^{j}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}\big\}-x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-{2\over s^{4}}(p_{1\mu}p_{2\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)\langle{\bar{\psi}}{\not\!\!A}(x){\not\!p}_{2}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}{\not\!\!B}(0){\not\!p}_{1}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-x\leftrightarrow 0

(169)

where again we used Eq. (
318
) and Eq. (
53
).
Similarly, the corresponding term in Eq. (
168
) is

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{\rm Eq.~(\ref{chew10a})}~=~{2i\over s^{5}}(p_{1\mu}p_{2\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)\big\{\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\sigma_{\star i}A^{j}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}j}\sigma_{\star{\bullet}}B^{i}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\sigma_{\star i}\sigma_{\star{\bullet}}A^{j}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}j}B^{i}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\big\}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~x\leftrightarrow 0~=~-{2\over s^{4}}(p_{1\mu}p_{2\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)\big\{\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(0)\sigma_{\star i}A^{j}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}j}B^{i}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt-~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\big\}-x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-{2\over s^{4}}(p_{1\mu}p_{2\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)\big\{\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(0)\sigma_{\star i}{1\over\alpha}A^{i}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}j}B^{j}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt+~\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(0)\sigma_{\star i}A^{j}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)[B^{i}(x)\sigma_{{\bullet}j}-B^{j}(x)\sigma_{{\bullet}i}]\psi(x)\rangle_{B}\big\}-x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-{2\over s^{4}}(p_{1\mu}p_{2\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\sigma_{\star i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{2\over s^{4}}(p_{1\mu}p_{2\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)\big\{\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(0){\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!\!A}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\beta}\big)(0){\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!\!B}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

(170)

Using QCD equations of motion from Sect.
9.3
we see that the contributions (
169
) and (
170
) cancel.

Now suppose
\mu
is longitudinal and
\nu
transverse

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{i\over s^{4}}\big\{p_{1\mu}\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{j}(x)\sigma_{\star i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{i}(0)\sigma_{\star\nu_{\perp}}\sigma_{{\bullet}j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}~-~p_{2\mu}\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{j}(x)\sigma_{{\bullet}\nu_{\perp}}\sigma_{\star i}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt\times~\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{i}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}j}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}-\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\big\}-x\leftrightarrow 0

(171)

Due to the first line in Eq. (
292
), both projectile and target matrix elements can bring factor
s
each,
so the result is either
\sim p_{1\mu}q^{\perp}_{\nu}{q_{\perp}^{2}\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}s^{2}}
or
\sim p_{2\mu}q^{\perp}_{\nu}{q_{\perp}^{2}\over\alpha_{q}\beta_{q}s^{2}}
, both of which are small in comparison to Eq. (
141
).

Let us now consider term coming from
{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}
and
\Xi_{1}
:

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{W}^{\rm as}_{\mu\nu}(x)~=~{i\over 2s^{3}}\big\{\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{2}\sigma_{\mu\nu}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)B^{i}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt-~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\big\}-x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{i\over s^{3}}p_{2\nu}\big\{\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\gamma_{i}\sigma_{\star\mu}\gamma_{j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)B^{i}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt-~\psi(0)\otimes\psi(x)\leftrightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\otimes\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\big\}~-~\mu\leftrightarrow\nu~-~x\leftrightarrow 0

(172)

Since nonzero contribution comes from transverse
\mu
, power counting for the r.h.s. of this equation gives
{p_{2\nu}q^{\perp}_{\mu}\over\alpha_{q}^{2}s^{2}}

(see Eq. (
330
)) which is
O\big({q_{\perp}^{2}\over\alpha_{q}s}\big)
in comparison Eq. (
141
). Similarly, the contribution
to
\check{W}^{\rm as}_{\mu\nu}(x)
coming from
{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}
and
\Xi_{2}
will be
\sim{p_{1\nu}q^{\perp}_{\mu}\over\beta_{q}^{2}s^{2}}

and hence negligible. Thus, we proved that
W^{\rm as}_{\mu\nu}=0
with our accuracy.

5.3.3
Exchange-type power corrections to
W^{\rm ZA}_{\mu\nu}

Power corrections of the “exchange” type come from the terms

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt(\check{W}^{\rm ZA}_{ff^{\prime}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over s}\langle A,B|{\cal J}_{1\mu}(x){\cal J}_{2\nu}(0)-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle-x\leftrightarrow 0

(173)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=\sum_{f,f^{\prime}}c_{f}c_{f^{\prime}}\Big(a_{f}a_{f^{\prime}}(\check{W}^{{\rm A}ff^{\prime}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)+(\check{W}^{{\rm A}ff^{\prime}}_{55})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)-a_{f}(\check{W}^{{\rm A}ff^{\prime}}_{5{\rm a}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)-a_{f^{\prime}}(\check{W}^{{\rm A}ff^{\prime}}_{5{\rm b}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)\Big)

where

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt(\check{W}^{\rm A})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over s}\langle A,B|[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Psi^{f}_{1}(x)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Psi^{f^{\prime}}_{2}(0)]-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle-x\leftrightarrow 0,

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt(\check{W}^{\rm A}_{55})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)~\equiv~{N_{c}\over s}\langle A,B|[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{1}(x)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{2}(0)]-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle-x\leftrightarrow 0,

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt(\check{W}^{\rm A}_{5{\rm a}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)~\equiv~{N_{c}\over s}\langle A,B|[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{1}(x)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Psi_{2}(0)]-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle-x\leftrightarrow 0,

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt(\check{W}^{\rm A}_{5{\rm b}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)~\equiv~{N_{c}\over s}\langle A,B|[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Psi_{1}(x)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{2}(0)]-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu|A,B\rangle-x\leftrightarrow 0

(174)

To avoid cluttering formulas, we will omit trivial flavor indices until final result.

Let us start from

(W^{\rm A})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)
. Since replacement of symmetrization in
\mu,\nu
by antisymmetrizaton
does not change power counting, we can start from analog of formula (
90
)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt(\check{W}^{\rm A})^{{\rm ex}}_{\mu\nu}(x)~=~{2N_{c}\over s}\langle p_{A},p_{B}|[{\bar{\Xi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi_{A}(x)+{\bar{\psi}}_{A}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\Xi_{1}(x)\big]

(175)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\big[{\bar{\Xi}}_{2}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi_{B}(0)+\bar{\psi}_{B}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Xi_{2}(0)\big]|p_{A},p_{B}\rangle-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{N_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)s^{3}}\Big(\langle\psi(x)\gamma_{\mu}\not{p}_{2}\gamma^{j}A_{k}(0){1\over\alpha}\psi(x)+\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\gamma^{j}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{\mu}A_{k}(0)\psi(x)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\not{p}_{1}\gamma^{k}B_{j}(x){1\over\beta}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}+\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\gamma^{k}\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{\nu}B_{j}(x)\psi_{B}(0)\rangle_{B}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-x\leftrightarrow 0

with transverse
\mu
and
\nu
. Using Eq. (
316
) we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt(\check{W}^{\rm A})^{{\rm ex}}_{\mu\nu}(x)~=~-{N_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)s^{3}}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\breve{A}_{\mu}(0)\not{p}_{2}\psi(x)+\bar{\psi}(x)\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{\mu}(0){1\over\alpha}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\breve{B}_{\nu}(x)\not{p}_{1}\psi(0)+\bar{\psi}(0)\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{\nu}(x){1\over\beta}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-x\leftrightarrow 0

(176)

and therefore

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt(W^{A})_{\mu\nu}^{\rm ex}(q)~=~-{N_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)Q_{\parallel}^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}[k^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu]

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt\times~[(j_{2}-{\bar{j}}_{2})(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\ast}_{2}-{\bar{j}}^{\ast}_{2})(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})-{\rm c.c.}]

(177)

where we used Eqs. (
9.4
) and (
9.4
). Since the functions
j_{i}(x,k_{\perp})
are actually
functions of
x
and
k_{\perp}^{2}
, the
r.h.s. of the above equationcan be proportional only to
g^{\perp}_{\mu\nu}
or
q^{\perp}_{\mu}q^{\perp}_{\nu}

and hence it vanishes

(W^{A})_{\mu\nu}^{\rm ex}(q)~=~0

(178)

Similarly to the symmetric case studied in Sect.
5.1.1
,
the replacement
\psi\rightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi
in the projectile matrix elements
leads to
k_{\mu}j_{1,}\rightarrow\pm i\epsilon_{\mu\nu}k^{\nu}j_{1,2}

and the replacement
\psi\rightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi
in target
matrix elements yields
(q-k)_{\mu}j_{1,2}\rightarrow\pm i\epsilon_{\mu}(q-k)^{\nu}j_{1,2}
.
Looking at the result (
177
)
and taking care of signs of replacements
\psi\rightarrow\gamma_{5}\psi

in parametrizations (
9.4
) and
(
9.4
), we obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt(\check{W}^{{\rm A}ff^{\prime}}_{55})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)=~-{N_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)s^{3}}\Big(\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\breve{A}_{\mu}(0)\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{5}\psi(x)+\bar{\psi}(x)\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{\mu}(0){1\over\alpha}\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}^{f}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt\times~\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\breve{B}_{\nu}(x)\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}+\bar{\psi}(0)\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{\nu}(x){1\over\beta}\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}^{f^{\prime}}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\Rightarrow~(W_{\mu\nu}^{{\rm A}ff^{\prime}})_{55}^{\rm ex}(q)~=~-{N_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)Q_{\parallel}^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}[k^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu]

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt\times~[(j_{2}+{\bar{j}}_{2})^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\ast}_{2}+{\bar{j}}^{\ast}_{2})^{f^{\prime}}(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})-{\rm c.c.}]~=~0

(179)

for the same reason as Eq. (
178
) above. The non-vanishing contributions come from

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt(\check{W}^{{\rm A}ff^{\prime}}_{5{\rm a}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)~=~-{N_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)s^{3}}\Big(\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\breve{A}_{\mu}(0)\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{5}\psi(x)+\bar{\psi}(x)\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{\mu}(0){1\over\alpha}\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}^{f}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\breve{B}_{\nu}(x)\not{p}_{1}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}+\bar{\psi}(0)\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{\nu}(x){1\over\beta}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}^{f^{\prime}}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-x\leftrightarrow 0

(180)

and

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt(\check{\cal W}^{{\rm A}ff^{\prime}}_{5{\rm b}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)~=~-{N_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)s^{3}}\Big(\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\breve{A}_{\mu}(0)\not{p}_{2}\psi(x)+\bar{\psi}(x)\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{\mu}(0){1\over\alpha}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}^{f}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\breve{B}_{\nu}(x)\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}+\bar{\psi}(0)\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{\nu}(x)\gamma_{5}{1\over\beta}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}^{f^{\prime}}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-x\leftrightarrow 0

(181)

Similarly to Eq. (
179
), from parametrizations (
9.4
) and
(
9.4
) one obtains

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt(W_{\mu\nu}^{{\rm A}ff^{\prime}})_{5{\rm a}}^{\rm ex}(q)~=~-{iN_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)Q_{\parallel}^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}[\epsilon_{\mu m}k^{m}(q-k)_{\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu]J^{2ff^{\prime}}_{+-}(q,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt(W_{\mu\nu}^{{\rm A}ff^{\prime}})_{5{\rm b}}^{\rm ex}(q)~=~{iN_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)Q_{\parallel}^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}[k_{\mu}\epsilon_{\nu n}(q-k)^{n}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu]J^{2ff^{\prime}}_{-+}(q,k_{\perp})

(182)

where
J^{2ff^{\prime}}_{+-}
and
J^{2ff^{\prime}}_{-+}
are defined in Eq. (
450
).

5.4
Results for antisymmetric hadronic tensor for Z-mediated DY process

The “annihilation” part is given by Eqs. (
123
) and (
151
)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW^{\rm ZAan}_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~-2i\epsilon_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}{q^{\alpha}\over Q_{\parallel}^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}({\tilde{q}}^{\beta}+q_{\perp}^{\beta}-2k_{\perp}^{\beta})\sum_{f}a_{f}c_{f}^{2}{\cal F}^{f}(q,k_{\perp})

(183)

It trivially satisfies
q^{\mu}W^{\rm ZAan}_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~0
.

The “exchange” part is given by the sum of contributions (
173
) calculated in previous Section

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW^{\rm ZAex}_{\mu\nu}(q)

\displaystyle~=~

\displaystyle\sum_{f,f^{\prime}}c_{f}c_{f^{\prime}}(W^{\rm ZA}_{ff^{\prime}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(q)

(184)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt(W^{\rm ZA}_{ff^{\prime}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(q)

\displaystyle~=~

\displaystyle{iN_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)Q_{\parallel}^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}\Big[a_{f}[\epsilon_{\mu m}k^{m}(q-k)_{\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu]J^{2ff^{\prime}}_{+-}(q,k_{\perp})

(185)

\displaystyle~-~

\displaystyle a_{f^{\prime}}[k_{\mu}\epsilon_{\nu n}(q-k)^{n}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu]J^{2ff^{\prime}}_{-+}(q,k_{\perp})\Big]

where
J^{2ff^{\prime}}_{+-}
and
J^{2ff^{\prime}}_{-+}
are defined in Eq. (
450
).

It can be parametrized as

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW^{\rm ZAex}_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~{iN_{c}\over(N_{c}^{2}-1)Q_{\parallel}^{2}}\sum_{f,f^{\prime}}c_{f}c_{f^{\prime}}

(186)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\times~\Big[\Big(\epsilon_{\mu\nu}+{\epsilon_{\mu m}q^{m}q^{\perp}_{\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu\over q_{\perp}^{2}}\Big)\big(a_{f}E^{2ff^{\prime}}_{+-}+a_{f^{\prime}}E^{2ff^{\prime}}_{-+}\big)-~\epsilon_{\mu\nu}\big(a_{f}{\cal E}^{2ff^{\prime}}_{+-}+a_{f^{\prime}}{\cal E}^{2ff^{\prime}}_{-+}\big)\Big]

As usually, the exchange part is non-zero in our approximation only for transverse indices.

6
Hadronic tensors for interference terms

6.1
Symmetric part of interference hadronic tensor
W^{\rm I1}

From definitions (
9
) and (
10
) we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{W}^{\rm I1S}_{\mu\nu}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over 2s}\sum_{f,f^{\prime}}\langle A,B|(e_{f^{\prime}}c_{f}a_{f}+e_{f}c_{f^{\prime}}a_{f^{\prime}})[{\bar{\psi}}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi(x)]^{f}[{\bar{\psi}}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi(0)]^{f^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt-~e_{f}c_{f^{\prime}}[{\bar{\psi}}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi(x)]^{f}[{\bar{\psi}}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}\psi(0)]^{f^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt-~e_{f^{\prime}}c_{f}[{\bar{\psi}}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\psi(x)]^{f}[{\bar{\psi}}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi(0)]^{f^{\prime}}|A,B\rangle+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu

(187)

where
\psi=\Psi_{1}+\Psi_{2}
in our approximation.

6.1.1
Annihilation-type terms

Let us start from the “annihilation” part

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{W}^{\rm I1San}_{\mu\nu}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over 2s}\sum_{f}e_{f}c_{f}\langle A,B|2a_{f}[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}^{f}\gamma_{\mu}\Psi_{2}^{f}(x)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}^{f}\gamma_{\nu}\Psi_{1}^{f}(0)]

(188)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}^{f}\gamma_{\mu}\Psi_{2}^{f}(x)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}^{f}\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{1}^{f}(0)]-[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}^{f}\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{2}^{f}(x)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}^{f}\gamma_{\nu}\Psi_{1}^{f}(0)]|A,B\rangle+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu+x\leftrightarrow 0

The first term can be copied from photon case (
24
) so we need to consider the last two terms. Using Fierz transformation (
286
) we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{N_{c}\over 2s}\langle A,B|[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}\gamma_{\mu}\Psi_{2}(x)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}\Psi(0)]+[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{2}(x)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}\gamma_{\nu}\Psi_{1}(0)]|A,B\rangle+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu+x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~\big(g_{\mu\nu}g^{\alpha\beta}-\delta_{\mu}^{\alpha}\delta_{\nu}^{\beta}-\delta_{\nu}^{\alpha}\delta_{\mu}^{\beta}\big){N_{c}\over 2s}\langle A,B|[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\alpha}\Psi_{1}(0)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{2}(x)]

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt+~[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}(x)\gamma_{\alpha}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{1}(0)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\Psi_{2}(x)]|A,B\rangle+x\leftrightarrow 0~=~\check{W}_{\mu\nu}^{\rm 5}(x)

Since we proved in Sect.
5.1
that
\check{W}_{\mu\nu}^{\rm 5}
is negligible (see Eq. (
105
)) we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{W}^{\rm I1San}_{\mu\nu}(x)~=~\sum_{f}e_{f}c_{f}a_{f}\check{W}^{f}(x)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\Rightarrow~W^{\rm I1San}_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~\sum_{f}e_{f}c_{f}a_{f}\big[W^{{\rm F}f}_{\mu\nu}(q)+W^{{\rm H}f}_{\mu\nu}(q)+W^{{\rm H}2f}_{\mu\nu}(q)\big]

(189)

where
W^{{\rm F}f}_{\mu\nu}
,
W^{{\rm H}f}_{\mu\nu}
, and
W^{{\rm H}2f}_{\mu\nu}
are given
by Eqs. (
49
), (
85
) , and (
87
), respectively.

6.1.2
Exchange-type power corrections

Let us consider now the exchange-type power corrections. From Eq. (
199
) we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{W}^{\rm I1Sex}_{\mu\nu}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over 2s}\sum_{f}\langle A,B|(e_{f^{\prime}}c_{f}a_{f}+e_{f}c_{f^{\prime}}a_{f^{\prime}})[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}^{f}\gamma_{\mu}\Psi_{1}^{f}(x)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}^{f^{\prime}}\gamma_{\nu}\Psi_{2}^{f^{\prime}}(0)]

(190)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~e_{f}c_{f^{\prime}}[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}^{f}\gamma_{\mu}\Psi_{1}^{f}(x)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}^{f^{\prime}}\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{2}^{f^{\prime}}(0)]

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt-~c_{f}e_{f^{\prime}}[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}^{f}\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{1}^{f}(x)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}^{f^{\prime}}\gamma_{\nu}\Psi_{2}^{f^{\prime}}(0)]|A,B\rangle+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu+x\leftrightarrow 0

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt=~\sum_{f,f^{\prime}}\Big({1\over 2}(e_{f^{\prime}}c_{f}a_{f}+e_{f}c_{f^{\prime}}a_{f^{\prime}})\check{W}^{ff^{\prime}{\rm ex}}_{\mu\nu}(x)-{e_{f^{\prime}}c_{f}\over 2}(\check{W}^{{\rm S}ff^{\prime}}_{5{\rm a}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)-{e_{f}c_{f^{\prime}}\over 2}(\check{W}^{{\rm S}ff^{\prime}}_{5{\rm b}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)\Big)

where
\check{W}^{ff^{\prime}{\rm ex}}_{\mu\nu}(x)
is defined by Eq. (
90
)
while
(\check{W}^{{\rm S}ff^{\prime}}_{5\rm a,b})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(x)
are defined in Eq. (
112
).
Thus,

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054ptW^{\rm I1Sex}_{\mu\nu}(q)=\sum_{f,f^{\prime}}\Big[{e_{f^{\prime}}c_{f}a_{f}+e_{f}c_{f^{\prime}}a_{f^{\prime}}\over 2}W^{ff^{\prime}{\rm ex}}_{\mu\nu}(q)-{e_{f^{\prime}}c_{f}\over 2}(W^{{\rm S}ff^{\prime}}_{5{\rm a}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(q)-{e_{f}c_{f^{\prime}}\over 2}(W^{{\rm S}ff^{\prime}}_{5{\rm b}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(q)\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt=~{N_{c}\over 2(N_{c}^{2}-1)Q_{\parallel}^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}k_{\perp}\sum_{f,f^{\prime}}\Big[(e_{f^{\prime}}c_{f}a_{f}+e_{f}c_{f^{\prime}}a_{f^{\prime}})\Big([k^{\perp}_{\mu}(q-k)^{\perp}_{\nu}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu+g^{\perp}_{\mu\nu}(k,q-k)_{\perp}]

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt\times~J^{1ff^{\prime}}_{--}(q,k_{\perp})-~g^{\perp}_{\mu\nu}(k,q-k)_{\perp}J^{2ff^{\prime}}_{--}(q,k_{\perp})\Big)

(191)

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt-~e_{f^{\prime}}c_{f}[\epsilon_{\mu m}k^{m}(q-k)_{\nu}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu]I^{1ff^{\prime}}_{+-}(q,k_{\perp})+~e_{f}c_{f^{\prime}}[k_{\mu}\epsilon_{\nu n}(q-k)^{n}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu]I^{1ff^{\prime}}_{-+}(q,k_{\perp})

where
W^{ff^{\prime}{\rm ex}}_{\mu\nu}(q)
,
(W^{{\rm S}ff^{\prime}}_{5{\rm a}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(q)
, and

(W^{{\rm S}ff^{\prime}}_{5{\rm b}})^{\rm ex}_{\mu\nu}(q)

are given in Eqs. (
99
), (
114
), and (
115
), respectively.

6.1.3
The result for the symmetric part of
W^{\rm I1}_{\mu\nu}(x)

The result for
W^{\rm I1S}_{\mu\nu}(q)
can be represented as a sum of “annihilation” and “exchange” parts

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW^{\rm I1S}_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~W^{\rm I1San}_{\mu\nu}(q)~+~W^{\rm I1Sex}_{\mu\nu}(q)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptW^{\rm I1San}_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~\sum_{f}e_{f}c_{f}a_{f}\big[W^{\rm Ff}_{\mu\nu}(q)+W^{\rm Hf}_{\mu\nu}(q)+W^{\rm H2f}_{\mu\nu}(q)\big]

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84526ptW^{\rm I1Sex}_{\mu\nu}(q)~=~{\rm r.h.s.~of~Eq.~(\ref{w1sexresult})}

(192)

where
W^{\rm Ff}_{\mu\nu}(q)
is given by Eq. (
49
),

W^{\rm Hf}_{\mu\nu}(q)
by Eq. (
84
), and
W^{\rm H2f}_{\mu\nu}(q)
by Eq. (
87
).

6.2
Antiymmetric part of interference hadronic tensor
W^{\rm I1}

From definitions (
9
) and (
10
) we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{W}^{\rm I1A}_{\mu\nu}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over 2s}\sum_{f,f^{\prime}}\langle A,B|(e_{f^{\prime}}c_{f}a_{f}+e_{f}c_{f^{\prime}}a_{f^{\prime}})[{\bar{\psi}}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi(x)]^{f}[{\bar{\psi}}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi(0)]^{f^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt-~e_{f}c_{f^{\prime}}[{\bar{\psi}}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\psi(x)]^{f}[{\bar{\psi}}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}\psi(0)]^{f^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt-~e_{f^{\prime}}c_{f}[{\bar{\psi}}(x)\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\psi(x)]^{f}[{\bar{\psi}}(0)\gamma_{\nu}\psi(0)]^{f^{\prime}}|A,B\rangle-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu

(193)

where
\psi=\Psi_{1}+\Psi_{2}
in our approximation.

Let us start from the annihilation part. After Fierz transformations (
284
) and (
288
) we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\check{W}^{\rm I1Aan}_{\mu\nu}(x)~=~{N_{c}\over 2s}\sum_{f}e_{f}c_{f}\langle A,B|2a_{f}[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}^{f}\gamma_{\mu}\Psi_{2}^{f}(x)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}^{f}\gamma_{\nu}\Psi_{1}^{f}(0)]

(194)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt-~[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}^{f}\gamma_{\mu}\Psi_{2}^{f}(x)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}^{f}\gamma_{\nu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{1}^{f}(0)]-[{\bar{\Psi}}_{1}^{f}\gamma_{\mu}\gamma_{5}\Psi_{2}^{f}(x)][{\bar{\Psi}}_{2}^{f}\gamma_{\nu}\Psi_{1}^{f}(0)]|A,B\rangle-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-x\leftrightarrow 0
