\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\sigma_{\mu\xi}\gamma_{5}\otimes\sigma_{\nu}^{~\xi}\gamma_{5}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-{g_{\mu\nu}\over 2}\sigma_{\xi\eta}\gamma_{5}\otimes\sigma^{\xi\eta}\gamma_{5}~=~-[\sigma_{\mu\xi}\otimes\sigma_{\nu}^{~\xi}+\mu\leftrightarrow\nu-{g_{\mu\nu}\over 2}\sigma_{\xi\eta}\otimes\sigma^{\xi\eta}]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\tilde{\sigma}_{\star j}\otimes\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bullet}k}~=~-{s\over 4}g_{jk}\sigma_{\xi\eta}\otimes\sigma^{\xi\eta}+{s\over 2}\sigma_{k\xi}\otimes\sigma_{j}^{~\xi}+g_{jk}\sigma_{{\bullet}\xi}\otimes\sigma_{\star}^{~\xi}-\sigma_{{\bullet}k}\otimes\sigma_{\star j}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-{s\over 4}g_{jk}\sigma_{mn}\otimes\sigma^{mn}-g_{jk}\sigma_{\star l}\otimes\sigma_{\bullet}^{~l}+\sigma_{\star k}\otimes\sigma_{{\bullet}j}+{s\over 2}\sigma_{kl}\otimes\sigma_{j}^{~l}

(295)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\sigma_{\star i}\otimes\sigma_{{\bullet}j}-\sigma_{\star i}\gamma_{5}\otimes\sigma_{{\bullet}j}\gamma_{5}

(296)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-g_{ij}^{\perp}\sigma_{\star l}\otimes\sigma_{\bullet}^{~l}+\sigma_{\star j}\otimes\sigma_{{\bullet}i}+\sigma_{\star i}\otimes\sigma_{{\bullet}j}-{s\over 4}g_{ij}^{\perp}\sigma_{mn}\otimes\sigma^{mn}+{s\over 2}\sigma_{jl}\otimes\sigma_{i}^{~l}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\sigma_{\star}^{~k}\otimes\gamma_{i}\sigma_{{\bullet}k}\gamma_{j}~=~{\hat{p}}_{2}\gamma^{k}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}\gamma_{k}\gamma_{j}~=~{\hat{p}}_{2}\gamma^{k}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}(g_{ik}\gamma_{j}+g_{jk}\gamma_{i}-g_{ij}\gamma_{k})

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{\hat{p}}_{2}(g_{ik}\gamma_{j}+g_{jk}\gamma_{i}-g_{ij}\gamma_{k})\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{k}~=~(\gamma_{j}\sigma_{\star}^{~k}\gamma_{i})\otimes\sigma_{{\bullet}k}

(297)

We will need also

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{j}+{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{j}\gamma_{5}~=~\gamma_{j}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}+\gamma_{j}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}\gamma_{5}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{5}

(298)

9.1.3
Formulas with
\gamma
-matrices and one gluon field

In the gauge
A_{\bullet}=0
the “projectile” field
A_{i}
can be represented as

A_{i}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})~=~{2\over s}\!\int_{-\infty}^{x_{\bullet}}\!dx^{\prime}_{\bullet}~F^{(A)}_{\star i}(x^{\prime}_{\bullet},x_{\perp})

(299)

and similarly for the “target” field

B_{i}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})~=~{2\over s}\!\int_{-\infty}^{x_{\star}}\!dx^{\prime}_{\star}~F^{(B)}_{\star i}(x^{\prime}_{\star},x_{\perp})

(300)

in the
B_{\bullet}=0
gauge. It is convenient to define

\tilde{A}_{i}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})~=~{2\over s}\!\int_{-\infty}^{x_{\bullet}}\!dx^{\prime}_{\bullet}~\tilde{F}^{(A)}_{\star i}(x^{\prime}_{\bullet},x_{\perp}),~~~\tilde{B}_{i}(x_{\star},x_{\perp})~=~{2\over s}\!\int_{-\infty}^{x_{\star}}\!dx^{\prime}_{\star}~\tilde{F}^{(B)}_{{\bullet}i}(x^{\prime}_{\star},x_{\perp}),

(301)

where
{\tilde{F}}_{\mu\nu}={1\over 2}\epsilon_{\mu\nu\lambda\rho}F^{\lambda\rho}
as usual.
With this definition we have
{\tilde{A}}_{i}=-\epsilon_{ij}A^{j}
and
{\tilde{B}}_{i}=\epsilon_{ij}B^{j}
so

{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}~=~-{\not\!\!A}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i},~~~~\breve{A}_{i}{\not\!p}_{2}~=~-\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!\!A},~~~~{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}_{i}~=~-{\not\!\!B}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i},~~~~\breve{B}_{i}{\not\!p}_{1}~=~-\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!\!B}

(302)

We also used

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptA^{i}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes\gamma_{n}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}+~A^{i}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{n}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}\gamma_{5}~=~-{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{n}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}-{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{n}\gamma_{5}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{5}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptA^{i}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{n}+~A^{i}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{n}\gamma_{5}~=~-\breve{A}_{n}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}-\breve{A}_{n}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{5}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{5}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\gamma_{n}\not{p}_{2}\gamma^{i}\otimes\not{p}_{1}B_{i}~+~\gamma_{n}\not{p}_{2}\gamma^{i}\gamma_{5}\otimes\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{5}B_{i}~=~-\not{p}_{2}\otimes\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{n}-\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{5}\otimes\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{n}\gamma_{5}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\gamma^{i}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{n}\otimes\not{p}_{1}B_{i}~+~\gamma^{i}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{n}\gamma_{5}\otimes\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{5}B_{i}~=~-\not{p}_{2}\otimes\breve{B}_{n}\not{p}_{1}-\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{5}\otimes\breve{B}_{n}\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{5}

(303)

and

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt{2\over s}\big[{\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}\otimes B^{i}\gamma_{n}+{\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}\gamma_{5}\otimes B^{i}\gamma_{n}\gamma_{5}\big]~=~\gamma_{i}\otimes\gamma_{n}\breve{B}_{i}+\gamma_{i}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{n}\breve{B}_{i}\gamma_{5}

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt{2\over s}\big[\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!p}_{1}\otimes B^{i}\gamma_{n}+\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{5}\otimes B^{i}\gamma_{n}\gamma_{5}\big]~=~\gamma_{i}\otimes\breve{B}_{i}\gamma_{n}+\gamma_{i}\gamma_{5}\otimes\breve{B}_{i}\gamma_{n}\gamma_{5}

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt{2\over s}({\hat{p}}_{2}\gamma^{i}{\hat{p}}_{1}\otimes{\hat{p}}_{1}B_{i}+{\hat{p}}_{2}\gamma^{i}{\hat{p}}_{1}\gamma_{5}\otimes{\hat{p}}_{1}\gamma_{5}B_{i})=-\gamma^{i}\otimes\breve{B}_{i}{\hat{p}}_{1}-\gamma^{i}\gamma_{5}\otimes\breve{B}_{i}{\hat{p}}_{1}\gamma_{5}

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt\gamma_{k}\gamma^{i}{\hat{p}}_{2}\otimes B_{i}\gamma^{k}+\gamma_{k}\gamma^{i}{\hat{p}}_{2}\gamma_{5}\otimes B_{i}\gamma^{k}\gamma_{5}~=~{\hat{p}}_{2}\otimes\gamma^{i}\breve{B}_{i}+{\hat{p}}_{2}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{i}\breve{B}^{i}\gamma_{5},

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt\not{p}_{2}\gamma^{j}\gamma^{i}\otimes\not{p}_{1}B_{j}~+~\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{5}\gamma^{i}\gamma^{j}\otimes\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{5}B_{j}=\not{p}_{2}\otimes\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{i}+\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{5}\otimes\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{5}\breve{B}^{i},

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt\not{p}_{2}\gamma^{i}\gamma^{j}\otimes\not{p}_{1}B_{j}~+~\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{5}\gamma^{i}\gamma^{j}\otimes\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{5}B_{j}=\not{p}_{2}\otimes\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}^{i}+\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{5}\otimes\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{5}\breve{B}^{i}.

(304)

9.1.4
Formulas with
\gamma
-matrices and two gluon fields

With definition (
301
), we have the following formulas

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptA_{i}\otimes{\tilde{B}}_{j}=g_{ij}{\tilde{A}}_{k}\otimes B^{k}-{\tilde{A}}_{j}\otimes B_{i},~~~\tilde{A}_{i}\otimes B_{j}=g_{ij}A_{k}\otimes{\tilde{B}}^{k}-A_{j}\otimes{\tilde{B}}_{i}

(305)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\tilde{A}_{i}\otimes{\tilde{B}}_{j}=-g_{ij}A_{k}\otimes B^{k}+A_{j}\otimes B_{i},~~~\Rightarrow~~~\tilde{A}_{i}\otimes\tilde{B}^{i}=-A_{i}\otimes B^{i},~~\tilde{A}_{i}\otimes B^{i}=A_{i}\otimes\tilde{B}^{i}

In addition, it is convenient to define

\breve{A}_{i}\equiv A_{i}-i{\tilde{A}}_{i}\gamma_{5},~~~~~~\breve{B}_{i}\equiv B_{i}-i{\tilde{B}}_{i}\gamma_{5}.

(306)

Using these formulas, after some algebra one obtains

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt\gamma_{m}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{j}A^{i}\otimes\gamma_{n}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}B^{j}+\gamma_{m}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{j}A^{i}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{n}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}B^{j}\gamma_{5}={\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{n}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}_{m}+{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{n}\gamma_{5}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}_{m}\gamma_{5}

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt\gamma_{j}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{m}A^{i}\otimes\gamma_{n}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}B^{j}+\gamma_{j}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{m}A^{i}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{n}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}B^{j}\gamma_{5}={\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{n}\otimes\breve{B}_{m}{\not\!p}_{1}+{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{n}\gamma_{5}\otimes\breve{B}_{m}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{5}

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt\gamma_{m}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{j}A^{i}\otimes\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{n}B^{j}+\gamma_{m}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{j}A^{i}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{n}B^{j}\gamma_{5}=\breve{A}_{n}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}_{m}+\breve{A}_{n}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{5}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}_{m}\gamma_{5}

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt\gamma_{j}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{m}A^{i}\otimes\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{n}B^{j}+\gamma_{j}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{m}A^{i}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{n}B^{j}\gamma_{5}=\breve{A}_{n}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes\breve{B}_{m}{\not\!p}_{1}+\breve{A}_{n}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{5}\otimes\breve{B}_{m}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{5}

(307)

and

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{m}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}_{n}+{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{n}\gamma_{5}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}_{m}\gamma_{5}~=~g_{mn}{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{k}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}^{k}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{m}\otimes\breve{B}_{n}{\not\!p}_{1}+{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{n}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{5}\breve{B}_{m}{\not\!p}_{1}~=~g_{mn}{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{k}\otimes\breve{B}^{k}{\not\!p}_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\breve{A}_{m}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}_{n}+\gamma_{5}\breve{A}_{n}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}_{m}\gamma_{5}~=~g_{mn}\breve{A}_{k}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}^{k}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\breve{A}_{m}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes\breve{B}_{n}{\not\!p}_{1}+\gamma_{5}\breve{A}_{n}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes\gamma_{5}\breve{B}_{m}{\not\!p}_{1}~=~g_{mn}\breve{A}_{k}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes\breve{B}^{k}{\not\!p}_{1}

(308)

The corollary of Eq. (
308
) is

\displaystyle\hskip-31.29802pt{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{k}\gamma_{5}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}^{k}\gamma_{5}~=~{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{k}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}^{k},~~~~~~~~{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{k}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{5}\breve{B}^{k}{\not\!p}_{1}~=~{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{k}\otimes\breve{B}^{k}{\not\!p}_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip-31.29802pt\gamma_{5}\breve{A}_{k}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}^{k}\gamma_{5}~=~\breve{A}_{k}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}^{k},~~~~~~~~\gamma_{5}\breve{A}_{k}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes\gamma_{5}\breve{B}^{k}{\not\!p}_{1}~=~\breve{A}_{k}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes\breve{B}^{k}{\not\!p}_{1}

(309)

From Eqs. (
307
) and (
308
) one easily obtains

\gamma_{m}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{j}A^{i}\otimes\gamma_{n}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}B^{j}+\gamma_{m}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{j}A^{i}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{n}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}B^{j}\gamma_{5}~+~m\leftrightarrow n~=~2g_{mn}{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{k}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}^{k}

(310)

and

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\gamma_{m}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{j}A^{i}\otimes\gamma_{n}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}B^{j}+\gamma_{m}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{j}A^{i}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{n}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}B^{j}\gamma_{5}~-~m\leftrightarrow n~

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~2{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{n}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}_{m}~-~m\leftrightarrow n,

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\gamma_{j}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{m}A^{i}\otimes\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{n}B^{j}+\gamma_{j}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{m}A^{i}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{n}B^{j}\gamma_{5}~-~m\leftrightarrow n~

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~2\breve{A}_{n}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes\breve{B}_{m}{\not\!p}_{1}~-~m\leftrightarrow n

(311)

We need also the formula

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{4\over s^{2}}A^{i}{\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{j}\otimes B^{j}{\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~A^{i}\gamma_{j}\otimes B^{j}\gamma_{i}-iA^{i}\gamma_{j}\gamma_{5}\otimes{\tilde{B}}^{j}\gamma_{i}+i{\tilde{A}}^{i}\gamma_{j}\otimes B^{j}\gamma_{i}\gamma_{5}+{\tilde{A}}^{i}\gamma_{j}\gamma_{5}\otimes{\tilde{B}}^{j}\gamma_{i}\gamma_{5}

(312)

and

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{4\over s^{2}}\big(A^{i}{\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{j}\otimes B^{j}{\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}+A^{i}{\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{j}\gamma_{5}\otimes B^{j}{\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}\gamma_{5}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~\gamma^{j}\breve{A}_{i}\otimes\gamma^{i}\breve{B}_{j}+\gamma^{j}\breve{A}_{i}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma^{i}\breve{B}_{j}\gamma_{5}

(313)

\gamma_{i}\breve{A}_{j}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{j}\breve{A}_{i}\gamma_{5}~=~\gamma_{i}\breve{A}_{j}\otimes\gamma^{i}\breve{B}^{j}-\gamma_{i}\breve{A}^{i}\otimes\gamma_{j}\breve{B}^{j}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptA_{k}\gamma_{i}\not{p}_{2}\gamma^{j}\otimes B_{j}\gamma^{i}\not{p}_{1}\gamma^{k}~=~\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}\otimes\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}^{i}~=~{\not\!\!A}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}\otimes{\not\!\!B}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i},

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptA_{k}\gamma^{j}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{i}\otimes B_{j}\gamma^{k}\not{p}_{1}\gamma^{i}~=~\breve{A}_{i}\not{p}_{2}\otimes\breve{B}_{i}\not{p}_{1}~=~\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!\!A}\otimes\gamma^{i}{\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!\!B},

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptA_{k}\gamma_{i}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{j}\otimes B^{j}\gamma^{k}\not{p}_{1}\gamma^{i}~=~\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}\otimes\breve{B}^{i}\not{p}_{1}~=~~{\not\!\!A}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}\otimes\gamma^{i}{\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!\!B},

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptA_{k}\gamma_{j}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{i}\otimes B^{j}\gamma^{i}\not{p}_{1}\gamma^{k}~=~\breve{A}_{i}\not{p}_{2}\otimes\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}^{i}~=~\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!\!A}\otimes{\not\!\!B}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}.

(314)

We used also

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptA^{k}\gamma_{m}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{j}\otimes B^{j}\gamma_{n}\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{k}+m\leftrightarrow n-g_{mn}A^{k}\gamma_{i}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{j}\otimes B^{j}\gamma^{i}\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{k}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~\breve{A}_{m}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes\breve{B}_{n}{\not\!p}_{1}+m\leftrightarrow n-g_{mn}\breve{A}_{k}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes\breve{B}^{k}{\not\!p}_{1},

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptA^{k}\gamma_{j}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{m}\otimes B^{j}\gamma_{n}\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{k}+m\leftrightarrow n-g_{mn}A^{k}\gamma_{j}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{i}\otimes B^{j}\gamma_{k}\not{p}_{1}\gamma^{i}~=~

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{m}\otimes\breve{B}_{n}{\not\!p}_{1}+m\leftrightarrow n-g_{mn}{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{k}\otimes\breve{B}^{k}{\not\!p}_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptA^{k}\gamma_{j}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{m}\otimes B^{j}\gamma_{k}\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{n}+m\leftrightarrow n-g_{mn}A^{k}\gamma_{j}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{i}\otimes B^{j}\gamma_{k}\not{p}_{1}\gamma^{i}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{m}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}_{n}+m\leftrightarrow n-g_{mn}{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{k}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}^{k},

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptA^{k}\gamma_{m}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{j}\otimes B^{j}\gamma_{k}\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{n}+m\leftrightarrow n-g_{mn}A^{k}\gamma_{j}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{i}\otimes B^{j}\gamma_{k}\not{p}_{1}\gamma^{i}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~\breve{A}_{m}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}_{n}+m\leftrightarrow n-g_{mn}\breve{A}_{k}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}^{k},

(315)

and

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\gamma^{j}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{m}A^{k}\otimes\gamma^{k}\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{n}B^{j}-m\leftrightarrow n~=~-\breve{A}_{m}\not{p}_{2}\otimes\breve{B}_{n}\not{p}_{1}-m\leftrightarrow n

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\gamma_{m}\not{p}_{2}\gamma^{j}A^{k}\otimes\gamma^{k}\not{p}_{1}\gamma_{n}B^{j}-m\leftrightarrow n~=~-\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{m}\otimes\breve{B}_{n}\not{p}_{1}-m\leftrightarrow n

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\gamma^{j}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{m}A^{k}\otimes\gamma_{n}\not{p}_{1}\gamma^{k}B^{j}-m\leftrightarrow n~=~-\breve{A}_{m}\not{p}_{2}\otimes\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{n}-m\leftrightarrow n

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\gamma_{m}\not{p}_{2}\gamma^{j}A^{k}\otimes\gamma_{n}\not{p}_{1}\gamma^{k}B^{j}-m\leftrightarrow n~=~-\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{m}\otimes\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{n}-m\leftrightarrow n

(316)

Next, using formula (
296
) we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptA^{i}\sigma_{\star j}\otimes B^{j}\sigma_{{\bullet}i}-A^{i}\sigma_{\star j}\gamma_{5}\otimes B^{j}\sigma_{{\bullet}i}\gamma_{5}

(317)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~({\bar{A}}^{j}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{k}-{\bar{A}}^{k}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{j})\otimes{\bar{B}}^{j}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{k}-\bar{\not\!\!A}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes\bar{\not\!\!B}{\not\!p}_{1}-{s\over 2}\bar{\not\!\!A}\gamma_{i}\otimes\bar{\not\!\!B}\gamma^{i}-{s\over 4}{\bar{A}}^{i}\sigma_{jk}\otimes{\bar{B}}_{i}\sigma^{jk}

We frequently use this formula in matrix elements like

\langle{\bar{\psi}}A^{i}(x)\sigma_{\star j}{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}\langle{\bar{\psi}}B^{j}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}i}{1\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}
. It is easy to see that in such matrix elements
the contribution of two last terms in Eq. (
317
) is
O\big({q_{\perp}^{2}\over s}\big)
in comparison to the first two ones
so for our purposes

\hskip 0.0ptA^{i}\sigma_{\star j}\otimes B^{j}\sigma_{{\bullet}i}-A^{i}\sigma_{\star j}\gamma_{5}\otimes B^{j}\sigma_{{\bullet}i}\gamma_{5}\simeq~({\bar{A}}^{j}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{k}-{\bar{A}}^{k}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{j})\otimes{\bar{B}}^{j}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{k}-\bar{\not\!\!A}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes\bar{\not\!\!B}{\not\!p}_{1}

(318)

We also need

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{4\over s^{2}}\big[A_{i}{\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{j}\otimes B_{j}{\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}+A_{i}{\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{j}\gamma_{5}\otimes B_{j}{\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}\gamma_{5}\big]~

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt=~\breve{A}_{i}\gamma_{j}\otimes\breve{B}_{j}\gamma_{i}+\breve{A}_{i}\gamma_{j}\gamma_{5}\otimes\breve{B}_{j}\gamma_{i}\gamma_{5}

(319)

and

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{2\over s}\big[A_{i}{\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{j}\otimes B_{j}\gamma_{n}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}+A_{i}{\not\!p}_{1}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{j}\gamma_{5}\otimes B_{j}\gamma_{\nu_{\perp}}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}\gamma_{5}\big]

(320)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-\gamma_{i}\breve{A}_{n}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}^{i}-\gamma_{i}\breve{A}_{n}\gamma_{5}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}^{i}\gamma_{5}~=~\gamma_{i}\breve{A}_{n}\otimes{\not\!\!B}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}+\gamma_{i}\breve{A}_{n}\gamma_{5}\otimes{\not\!\!B}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}\gamma_{5},

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{2\over s}\big[A_{i}\gamma_{n}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{j}\otimes B_{j}{\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}+A_{i}\gamma_{n}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma^{j}\gamma_{5}\otimes B_{j}{\not\!p}_{2}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma^{i}\gamma_{5}\big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=-{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}\otimes\gamma^{i}\breve{B}_{n}-{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma^{i}\breve{B}_{n}\gamma_{5}~=~{\not\!\!A}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}\otimes\gamma^{i}\breve{B}_{n}+{\not\!\!A}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{i}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma^{i}\breve{B}_{n}\gamma_{5}.

The last formula which we need is

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt\gamma_{m}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{j}A^{i}\otimes\gamma_{n}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}B^{j}+\gamma_{m}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{j}A^{i}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{n}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}B^{j}\gamma_{5}={\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{n}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}_{m}+{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{n}\gamma_{5}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}_{m}\gamma_{5}

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt\gamma_{j}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{m}A^{i}\otimes\gamma_{n}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}B^{j}+\gamma_{j}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{m}A^{i}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{n}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{i}B^{j}\gamma_{5}={\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{n}\otimes\breve{B}_{m}{\not\!p}_{1}+{\not\!p}_{2}\breve{A}_{n}\gamma_{5}\otimes\breve{B}_{m}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{5}

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt\gamma_{m}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{j}A^{i}\otimes\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{n}B^{j}+\gamma_{m}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{j}A^{i}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{n}B^{j}\gamma_{5}=\breve{A}_{n}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}_{m}+\breve{A}_{n}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{5}\otimes{\not\!p}_{1}\breve{B}_{m}\gamma_{5}

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt\gamma_{j}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{m}A^{i}\otimes\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{n}B^{j}+\gamma_{j}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{m}A^{i}\gamma_{5}\otimes\gamma_{i}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{n}B^{j}\gamma_{5}=\breve{A}_{n}{\not\!p}_{2}\otimes\breve{B}_{m}{\not\!p}_{1}+\breve{A}_{n}{\not\!p}_{2}\gamma_{5}\otimes\breve{B}_{m}{\not\!p}_{1}\gamma_{5}

(321)

9.2
Parametrization of leading-twist matrix elements

Let us first consider matrix elements of operators without
\gamma_{5}
. The standard parametrization of quark TMDs reads

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 16\pi^{3}}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}~\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}_{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\gamma^{\mu}{\hat{\psi}}_{f}(0)\rangle_{A}

(322)

\displaystyle\hskip 76.82243pt=~p_{1}^{\mu}f_{1}^{f}(\alpha,k_{\perp})+k_{\perp}^{\mu}f_{\perp}^{f}(\alpha,k_{\perp})+p_{2}^{\mu}{2m^{2}_{N}\over s}f_{3}^{f}(\alpha,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 16\pi^{3}}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}~\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}_{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp}){\hat{\psi}}_{f}(0)\rangle_{A}~=~m_{N}e^{f}(\alpha,k_{\perp})

for quark distributions in the projectile and

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 16\pi^{3}}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}~\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}_{f}(0)\gamma^{\mu}{\hat{\psi}}_{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}

(323)

\displaystyle\hskip 76.82243pt=~-p_{1}^{\mu}{\bar{f}}_{1}^{f}(\alpha,k_{\perp})-k_{\perp}^{\mu}{\bar{f}}_{\perp}^{f}(\alpha,k_{\perp})-p_{2}^{\mu}{2m^{2}_{N}\over s}{\bar{f}}_{3}^{f}(\alpha,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 16\pi^{3}}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}~\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}_{f}(0){\hat{\psi}}_{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}~=~m_{N}{\bar{e}}^{f}(\alpha,k_{\perp})

for the antiquark distributions.

11
11

11

In a general gauge for
projectile and target fields these matrix elements read

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt\langle{\hat{\psi}}_{f}(x)\gamma_{\mu}{\hat{\psi}}_{f}(0)\rangle_{A}~=~\langle{\hat{\psi}}_{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\gamma_{\mu}[x_{\bullet},-\infty_{\bullet}]_{x}[x_{\perp},0_{\perp}]_{-\infty_{\bullet}}[-\infty_{\bullet},0_{\bullet}]_{0}{\hat{\psi}}_{f}(0)\rangle_{A},

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt\langle{\hat{\psi}}_{f}(x)\gamma_{\mu}{\hat{\psi}}_{f}(0)\rangle_{B}~=~\langle{\hat{\psi}}_{f}(x_{\ast},x_{\perp})\gamma_{\mu}[x_{\ast},-\infty_{\ast}]_{x}[x_{\perp},0_{\perp}]_{-\infty_{\ast}}[-\infty_{\ast},0_{\ast}]_{0}{\hat{\psi}}_{f}(0)\rangle_{B}

(324)

and similarly for other operators.

The corresponding matrix elements for the target are obtained by trivial replacements
p_{1}\leftrightarrow p_{2}
,
x_{\bullet}\leftrightarrow x_{\star}

and
\alpha\leftrightarrow\beta
:

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 16\pi^{3}}\!\int\!dx_{\star}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\beta x_{\star}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}~\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}_{f}(x_{\star},x_{\perp})\gamma^{\mu}{\hat{\psi}}_{f}(0)\rangle_{B}

(325)

\displaystyle\hskip 76.82243pt=~p_{2}^{\mu}f_{1}^{f}(\beta,k_{\perp})+k_{\perp}^{\mu}f_{\perp}^{f}(\beta,k_{\perp})+p_{1}^{\mu}{2m^{2}_{N}\over s}f_{3}^{f}(\beta,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 16\pi^{3}}\!\int\!dx_{\star}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\beta x_{\star}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}~\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}_{f}(x_{\star},x_{\perp}){\hat{\psi}}_{f}(0)\rangle_{B}~=~m_{N}e^{f}(\beta,k_{\perp}),

and

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 16\pi^{3}}\!\int\!dx_{\star}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\beta x_{\star}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}~\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}_{f}(0)\gamma^{\mu}{\hat{\psi}}_{f}(x_{\star},x_{\perp})\rangle_{B}

(326)

\displaystyle\hskip 76.82243pt=~-p_{2}^{\mu}{\bar{f}}_{1}^{f}(\beta,k_{\perp})-k_{\perp}^{\mu}{\bar{f}}_{\perp}^{f}(\beta,k_{\perp})-p_{1}^{\mu}{2m^{2}_{N}\over s}{\bar{f}}_{3}^{f}(\beta,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 16\pi^{3}}\!\int\!dx_{\star}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\beta x_{\star}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}~\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}_{f}(0){\hat{\psi}}_{f}(x_{\star},x_{\perp})\rangle_{B}~=~m_{N}{\bar{e}}^{f}(\beta,k_{\perp}).

Matrix elements of operators with
\gamma_{5}
are parametrized as follows:

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 16\pi^{3}}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}~\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}_{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\gamma^{\mu}\gamma_{5}{\hat{\psi}}_{f}(0)\rangle_{A}~=~-i\epsilon_{\mu_{\perp}i}k^{i}g^{\perp}_{f}(\alpha,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 16\pi^{3}}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}~\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}_{f}(0)\gamma^{\mu}\gamma_{5}{\hat{\psi}}_{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}~=~-i\epsilon_{\mu_{\perp}i}k^{i}{\bar{g}}^{\perp}_{f}(\alpha,k_{\perp})

(327)

The corresponding matrix elements for the target are obtained by trivial replacements
p_{1}\leftrightarrow p_{2}
,
x_{\bullet}\leftrightarrow x_{\star}

and
\alpha\leftrightarrow\beta
similarly to eq. (
326
).

The parametrization of time-odd Boer-Mulders TMDs are

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 16\pi^{3}}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}~\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}_{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\sigma^{\mu\nu}{\hat{\psi}}_{f}(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt=~{1\over m_{N}}(k_{\perp}^{\mu}p_{1}^{\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)h_{1f}^{\perp}(\alpha,k_{\perp})+{2m_{N}\over s}(p_{1}^{\mu}p_{2}^{\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)h_{f}(\alpha,k_{\perp})

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt+~{2m_{N}\over s}(k_{\perp}^{\mu}p_{2}^{\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu)h_{3f}^{\perp}(\alpha,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 16\pi^{3}}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}~\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}_{f}(0)\sigma^{\mu\nu}{\hat{\psi}}_{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt=~-{1\over m_{N}}(k_{\perp}^{\mu}p_{1}^{\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu){\bar{h}}_{1f}^{\perp}(\alpha,k_{\perp})-{2m_{N}\over s}(p_{1}^{\mu}p_{2}^{\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu){\bar{h}}_{f}(\alpha,k_{\perp})

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt-~{2m_{N}\over s}(k_{\perp}^{\mu}p_{2}^{\nu}-\mu\leftrightarrow\nu){\bar{h}}_{3f}^{\perp}(\alpha,k_{\perp})

(328)

and similarly for the target with usual replacements
p_{1}\leftrightarrow p_{2}
,
x_{\bullet}\leftrightarrow x_{\star}

and
\alpha\leftrightarrow\beta
.

Note that
the coefficients in front of
f_{3}
,
g^{\perp}_{f}
,
h
and
h_{3}^{\perp}
in eqs. (
322
), (
325
), (
327
), and (
328
)
contain an extra
{1\over s}
since
p_{2}^{\mu}
enters only through the direction
of gauge link so the result should not depend on rescaling
p_{2}\rightarrow\lambda p_{2}
. For this reason, these functions do not contribute to
W(q)
in our approximation.

Last but not least, an important point in our analysis is that any
f(x,k_{\perp})
may have only logarithmic dependence on Bjorken
x
but
not the power dependence
\sim{1\over x}
. Indeed, at small
x
the cutoff of corresponding longitudinal integrals comes from
the rapidity cutoff
\sigma_{a}
, see footnote
12
and corresponding discussion in Ref.
[
16
]
. Thus, at small
x
one can safely put
x=0
and the corresponding
logarithmic contributions would be proportional to powers of
\alpha_{s}\ln\sigma_{a}
(or, in some cases,
\alpha_{s}\ln^{2}\sigma_{a}
, see e.g.
ref.
[
35
]
).

9.3
Matrix elements of quark-quark-gluon operators

In this section we will demonstrate that matrix elements of quark-antiquark-gluon operators
from section
4
can be expressed in terms of leading-power
matrix elements from section
9.2
. First, let us note that operators
{1\over\alpha}
and
{1\over\beta}
in Eqs. (
17
) are
replaced by
\pm{1\over\alpha_{q}}
and
\pm{1\over\beta_{q}}
in forward matrix elements. Indeed

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}}\langle\bar{\Phi}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\Gamma{1\over\alpha+i\epsilon}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

(329)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-i\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}\!\int_{-\infty}^{0}\!\!\!dx^{\prime}_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}}\langle\bar{\Phi}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\Gamma\psi(x^{\prime}_{\bullet},0_{\perp})\rangle_{A}={1\over\alpha_{q}}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}}\langle\bar{\Phi}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\Gamma\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

where
\bar{\Phi}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})
can be
{\bar{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})
or
{\bar{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})A_{i}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})
and
\Gamma
can be any
\gamma
-matrix.
Similarly,

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp}){1\over\alpha-i\epsilon}\big)\Gamma\Phi(0)\rangle_{A}=~{1\over\alpha_{q}}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}}\langle\bar{\psi}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\Gamma\Phi(0)\rangle_{A}

(330)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}{1\over\alpha-i\epsilon}\big)(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\Gamma{1\over\alpha+i\epsilon}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}=~{1\over\alpha_{q}^{2}}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}}\langle\bar{\psi}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\Gamma\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

where
\Phi(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})
can be
\psi(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})
or
A_{i}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\psi(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})
. We need also

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692pt\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}}\langle\big(\bar{\psi}(0){1\over\alpha-i\epsilon}\big)\Gamma\Phi(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}=~-{1\over\alpha_{q}}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}}\langle\bar{\psi}()\Gamma\Phi(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91692pt\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}}\langle\bar{\Phi}(0)\Gamma{1\over\alpha+i\epsilon}\psi(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}=~-{1\over\alpha_{q}}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}}\langle\bar{\Phi}(0)\Gamma\psi(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}

(331)

The corresponding formulas for target matrix elements are obtained by substitution
\alpha\leftrightarrow\beta
(and
x_{\bullet}\leftrightarrow x_{\star}
).

Next, we will use QCD equation of motion to reduce quark-quark-gluon TMDs to leading-twist TMDs (cf. Ref.
[
20
]
).
Let us start with matrix element

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\bar{\psi}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

(332)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\bar{\psi}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp}){\not\!\!A}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{i}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}

\displaystyle\hskip 88.20354pt\times~\big[\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{k}_{\perp}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{i}{\hat{\psi}}(0)\rangle_{A}+i\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\!\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\leftarrow}}{{{\not\!\!D}}}_{\perp}\!\gamma_{j}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{i}{\hat{\psi}}(0)\rangle_{A}\big].

Using QCD equations of motion we can rewrite the r.h.s. of eq. (
332
) as

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\big[\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{k}_{\perp}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{i}{\hat{\psi}}(0)\rangle_{A}+\alpha_{q}\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{1}\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{i}{\hat{\psi}}(0)\rangle_{A}\big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\Big[-k_{i}\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}{\hat{\psi}}(0)\rangle_{A}+~\alpha_{q}{s\over 2}\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\gamma_{i}{\hat{\psi}}(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.07164pt-~i\epsilon_{ij}k^{j}\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{5}{\hat{\psi}}(0)\rangle_{A}+i{s\over 2}\alpha\epsilon_{ij}\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\gamma^{j}\gamma_{5}{\hat{\psi}}(0)\rangle_{A}\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 39.83385pt=~-k_{i}8\pi^{3}sf_{1}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})+8\pi^{3}s\alpha_{q}k_{i}\big[f_{\perp}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})+g^{\perp}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})\big],

(333)

where we used parametrizations (
322
) and (
327
) for the leading power matrix elements.

Now, the second term in eq. (
333
) contains extra
\alpha_{q}
with respect to the first term

12
12

12

It can be demonstrated that
f_{1}(x,k_{\perp}^{2})
,
f_{\perp}(x,k_{\perp}^{2})
, and
f_{\perp}(x,k_{\perp}^{2})
have the same type of (logarithmic) behavior at low
x
.
Indeed, the low-
x
behavior is determined by interaction with pomeron. This interaction is specified by so-called impact factor
and it is easy to check that the impact factor for all three TMDs is similar.

, so
it should be neglected in our kinematical region
s\gg Q^{2}\gg q_{\perp}^{2}
and we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{g\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\bar{\psi}^{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\psi^{f}(0)\rangle_{A}

(334)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{-g\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\bar{\psi}^{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp}){\not\!\!A}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{i}\psi^{f}(0)\rangle_{A}~=-k_{i}f_{1}^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})

By complex conjugation

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{g\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\hat{{\bar{\psi}}}_{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\breve{A}_{i}(0)\not{p}_{2}{\hat{\psi}}_{f}(0)\rangle_{A}

(335)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{-g\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\bar{\psi}^{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\gamma_{i}\not{p}_{2}{\not\!\!A}(0)\psi^{f}(0)\rangle_{A}=~-k_{i}f_{1f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp}).

For the corresponding antiquark distributions we get

\displaystyle\hskip 0.0pt{g\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}_{f}(0)\breve{A}_{i}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}{\hat{\psi}}_{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=~{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\Big[-\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}(0)\gamma_{i}\not{p}_{2}\not{k}_{\perp}\hat{\psi}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt-~i\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}(0)\gamma_{i}\not{p}_{2}{\not\!\!D}_{\perp}{\hat{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}\Big]~=~-k_{i}{\bar{f}}_{1f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})

(336)

and

\hskip 0.0pt{g\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\hat{{\bar{\psi}}}_{f}(0)\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}(0){\hat{\psi}}_{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}~=~-k_{i}{\bar{f}}_{1f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp}).

(337)

The corresponding target matrix elements are obtained by trivial replacements

x_{\star}\leftrightarrow x_{\bullet}
,
\alpha_{q}\leftrightarrow\beta_{q}
and

\not{p}_{2}\leftrightarrow\not{p}_{1}
.

Next, let us consider

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{g\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\hat{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}{\not\!\!A}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp}){\hat{\psi}}(0)\rangle_{A}

(338)

\displaystyle\hskip 31.29802pt=~{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt\times~\Big[\langle{\hat{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{k}_{\perp}\not{p}_{2}{\hat{\psi}}(0)\rangle_{A}+i\langle{\hat{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\leftarrow}}{{{\not\!\!D}}}_{\perp}\not{p}_{2}{\hat{\psi}}(0)\rangle_{A}\Big].

Using QCD equation of motion and parametrization (
328
), one can rewrite the r.h.s. of this equation as

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\Big[\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{k}_{\perp}\not{p}_{2}{\hat{\psi}}(0)\rangle_{A}+\alpha_{q}\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{1}\not{p}_{2}{\hat{\psi}}(0)\rangle_{A}\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~i{k_{\perp}^{2}\over m_{N}}h_{1}^{\perp}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})+\alpha_{q}m_{N}\big[e(\alpha,k_{\perp})+ih(\alpha,k_{\perp})\big].

(339)

Again, only the first term contributes in our kinematical region so we finally get

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt{g\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}^{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}{\not\!\!A}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp}){\hat{\psi}}^{f}(0)\rangle_{A}~=~i{k_{\perp}^{2}\over m_{N}}h_{1f}^{\perp}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp}).

(340)

By complex conjugation we obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{g\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}^{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}{\not\!\!A}(0){\hat{\psi}}^{f}(0)\rangle_{A}~=~i{k_{\perp}^{2}\over m_{N}}h_{1f}^{\perp}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp}).

(341)

For corresponding antiquark distributions one gets in a similar way

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt{g\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}^{f}(0)\not{p}_{2}{\not\!\!A}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp}){\hat{\psi}}^{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}=~i{k_{\perp}^{2}\over m_{N}}{\bar{h}}_{1f}^{\perp}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-5.69054pt{g\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}^{f}(0)\not{p}_{2}{\not\!\!A}(0){\hat{\psi}}^{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}=~i{k_{\perp}^{2}\over m_{N}}{\bar{h}}_{1f}^{\perp}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp}).

(342)

The target matrix elements are obtained by usual replacements

x_{\star}\leftrightarrow x_{\bullet}
,
\alpha_{q}\leftrightarrow\beta_{q}
and

\not{p}_{2}\leftrightarrow\not{p}_{1}
.

For the Z-boson hadronic tensor we need these operators with extra
\gamma_{5}
. From formula (
333
) we see that

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\bar{\psi}^{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\gamma_{5}\psi^{f}(0)\rangle_{A}=-i\epsilon_{ij}k^{j}f_{1}^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\bar{\psi}^{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\breve{A}_{i}(0)\not{p}_{2}\gamma_{5}\psi^{f}(0)\rangle_{A}=~i\epsilon_{ij}k^{j}f_{1}^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})

(343)

Finally, we need

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\hat{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp}){\not\!\!A}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}{\not\!\!A}(0){\hat{\psi}}(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84526pt=~{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\hat{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\Big(\not{k}_{\perp}+i\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\leftarrow}}{{{\not\!\!D}}}\big)\not{p}_{2}\big(\not{k}_{\perp}-i{{\not\!\!D}}\big)\hat{\psi}(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt=~{k_{\perp}^{2}\over 16\pi^{3}}f_{1}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})~+~O(\alpha_{q},\beta_{q})

(344)

and similarly

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\hat{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp}){\not\!\!A}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\sigma_{\star i}{\not\!\!A}(0){\hat{\psi}}(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84526pt=~{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\hat{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\Big(\not{k}_{\perp}+i\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\leftarrow}}{{{\not\!\!D}}}\big)\sigma_{\star i}\big(\not{k}_{\perp}-i{{\not\!\!D}}\big)\hat{\psi}(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip 2.84526pt=~{1\over 16\pi^{3}}{k_{i}k_{\perp}^{2}\over m}h_{1}^{\perp}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})~+~O(\alpha_{q},\beta_{q})

(345)

For corresponding antiquark distributions we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{g\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\hat{\psi}}(0){\not\!\!A}(0)\not{p}_{2}{\not\!\!A}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp}){\hat{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt=~-{k_{\perp}^{2}\over 16\pi^{3}}{\bar{f}}_{1}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})~+~O(\alpha_{q},\beta_{q})

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{g\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}dx_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha_{q}x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\hat{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp}){\not\!\!A}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\sigma_{\star i}{\not\!\!A}(0){\hat{\psi}}(0)\rangle_{A}

\displaystyle\hskip 62.59605pt=~-{1\over 16\pi^{3}}{k_{i}k_{\perp}^{2}\over m}h_{1}^{\perp}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})~+~O(\alpha_{q},\beta_{q})

(346)

Also, as we saw in Sect.
4.3.2
, at the leading order in
N_{c}
there is one quark-antiquark-gluon operator that does not reduce to twist-2 distributions. It can be parametrized as follows

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 16\pi^{3}}{2\over s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}~\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}_{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\big[A_{i}(x)\sigma_{\star j}-{1\over 2}g_{ij}A^{k}\sigma_{\star k}(x)\big]{\hat{\psi}}_{f}(0)\rangle_{A}~

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt=~-(k_{i}k_{j}+{1\over 2}g_{ij}k_{\perp}^{2}){1\over m}h_{A}^{f}(\alpha,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 16\pi^{3}}{2\over s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}d^{2}x_{\perp}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}~\langle\hat{\bar{\psi}}_{f}(0)[A_{i}(0)\sigma_{{\bullet}j}-{1\over 2}g_{ij}A^{k}\sigma_{{\bullet}k}(0)]{\hat{\psi}}_{f}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}~

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt=~-(k_{i}k_{j}+{1\over 2}g_{ij}k_{\perp}^{2}){1\over m}{\bar{h}}_{A}^{f}(\alpha,k_{\perp})

(347)

and similarly for the target matrix element.

9.4
Matrix elements for exchange-type power corrections

We parametrize “exchange” TMDs as follows

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 4\pi^{3}s^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\!\int_{-\infty}^{x_{\bullet}}\!dx^{\prime}_{\bullet}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\breve{F}_{\star i}(0)\not{p}_{2}\psi(x^{\prime}_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}~=~k_{i}j_{1}(\alpha,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 4\pi^{3}s^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\!\int_{-\infty}^{x_{\bullet}}\!dx^{\prime}_{\bullet}~\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}\breve{F}_{\star i}(0)\psi(x^{\prime}_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}~=~k_{i}j_{2}(\alpha,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 4\pi^{3}s^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\!\int_{-\infty}^{x_{\bullet}}\!dx^{\prime}_{\bullet}~\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x^{\prime}_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}\breve{F}_{\star i}(0)\psi(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}~=~k_{i}{\bar{j}}_{1}(\alpha,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 4\pi^{3}s^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\!\int_{-\infty}^{x_{\bullet}}\!dx^{\prime}_{\bullet}~\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x^{\prime}_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\breve{F}_{\star i}(0)\not{p}_{2}\psi(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}~=~k_{i}{\bar{j}}_{2}(\alpha,k_{\perp})

(348)

where
\breve{F}_{\mu\nu}~\equiv~F_{\mu\nu}-i\gamma_{5}\tilde{F}_{\mu\nu}
.
By complex conjugation we get

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 4\pi^{3}s^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\!\int_{-\infty}^{0}\!dx^{\prime}_{\bullet}~\langle\bar{\psi}(x^{\prime}_{\bullet},0_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}\breve{F}_{\star i}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\psi(0)\rangle_{A}~=~k_{i}j_{1}^{\ast}(\alpha,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 4\pi^{3}s^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\!\int_{-\infty}^{0}\!dx^{\prime}_{\bullet}~\langle\bar{\psi}(x^{\prime}_{\bullet},0_{\perp})\breve{F}_{\star i}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}~=~k_{i}j_{2}^{\ast}(\alpha,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 4\pi^{3}s^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\!\int_{-\infty}^{0}\!dx^{\prime}_{\bullet}\langle\bar{\psi}(0)\breve{F}_{\star i}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\not{p}_{2}\psi(x^{\prime}_{\bullet},0_{\perp})\rangle_{A}~=~k_{i}{\bar{j}}_{1}^{\ast}(\alpha,k_{\perp}),

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 4\pi^{3}s^{2}}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\!\int_{-\infty}^{0}\!dx^{\prime}_{\bullet}\langle\bar{\psi}(0)\not{p}_{2}\breve{F}_{\star i}(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp}))\psi(x^{\prime}_{\bullet},0_{\perp})\rangle_{A}~=~k_{i}{\bar{j}}_{2}^{\ast}(\alpha,k_{\perp})

(349)

Note that unlike two-quark matrix elements,
quark-quark-gluon ones may have imaginary parts.
Target matrix elements are obtained by usual substitutions

\alpha\leftrightarrow\beta
,
\not{p}_{2}\leftrightarrow\not{p}_{1}
,
x_{\bullet}\leftrightarrow x_{\star}
, and
\breve{F}_{\star i}\leftrightarrow\breve{F}_{{\bullet}i}
.

13
13

13

For completeness let us present the explicit form of the gauge links in an arbitrary gauge:

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt{\bar{\psi}}(x^{\prime}_{\bullet},x_{\perp})F_{\star i}(0)\psi(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})~\rightarrow~{\bar{\psi}}(x^{\prime}_{\bullet},x_{\perp})[x^{\prime}_{\bullet},-\infty_{\bullet}]_{x}[x_{\perp},0_{\perp}]_{-\infty_{\bullet}}

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\times~[-\infty_{\bullet},0]_{0_{\perp}}F_{\star i}(0)[0,-\infty_{\bullet}]_{0_{\perp}}[0_{\perp},x_{\perp}]_{-\infty_{\bullet}}[-\infty_{\bullet},x_{\bullet}]_{x}\psi(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp}).

Let us now consider the corresponding matrix elements from Sect.
4.4
. As shown in Ref.
[
16
]
,
Eqs. (
348
) and (
349
) lead to (here
x=(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})
)

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x)\bigg[\begin{array}[]{c}\breve{A}_{i}(0)\not{p}_{2}\\
\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}(0)\end{array}\bigg]{1\over\alpha}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}~=~{k_{i}\over\alpha}\Big[\begin{array}[]{c}j_{1}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\\
j_{2}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\end{array}\Big],

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\bigg[\begin{array}[]{c}\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}(0)\\
\breve{A}_{i}(0)\not{p}_{2}\end{array}\bigg]\psi(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}~=~-{k_{i}\over\alpha}\Big[\begin{array}[]{c}{\bar{j}}_{1}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\\
{\bar{j}}_{2}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\end{array}\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)\bigg[\begin{array}[]{c}\breve{A}_{i}(x)\not{p}_{2}\\
\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}(x)\end{array}\bigg]{1\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}~=~-{k_{i}\over\alpha}\Big[\begin{array}[]{c}{\bar{j}}_{1}^{\ast}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\\
{\bar{j}}_{2}^{\ast}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\end{array}\Big],

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(0)\bigg[\begin{array}[]{c}\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}(x)\\
\breve{A}_{i}(x)\not{p}_{2}\end{array}\bigg]\psi(0)\rangle_{A}~=~{k_{i}\over\alpha}\Big[\begin{array}[]{c}j_{1}^{\ast}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\\
j_{2}^{\ast}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\end{array}\Big]

For the target matrix elements, we obtain (
x\equiv x_{\star},x_{\perp}
)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\star}~e^{-i\beta x_{\star}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x)\bigg[\begin{array}[]{c}\breve{B}_{i}(0)\not{p}_{1}\\
\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{i}(0)\end{array}\bigg]{1\over\beta}\psi(x\rangle_{B}~=~{k_{i}\over\beta}\Big[\begin{array}[]{c}j_{1}(\beta,k_{\perp})\\
j_{2}(\beta,k_{\perp})\end{array}\Big],

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\star}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\star}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\beta}\big)(x)\bigg[\begin{array}[]{c}\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{i}(0)\\
\breve{B}_{i}(0)\not{p}_{1}\end{array}\bigg]\psi(x)\rangle_{B}~=~-{k_{i}\over\beta}\Big[\begin{array}[]{c}{\bar{j}}_{1}(\beta,k_{\perp})\\
{\bar{j}}_{2}(\beta,k_{\perp})\end{array}\Big],

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\star}~e^{-i\beta x_{\star}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)\bigg[\begin{array}[]{c}\breve{B}_{i}(x)\not{p}_{1}\\
\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{i}(x)\end{array}\bigg]{1\over\beta}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}~=~-{k_{i}\over\beta}\Big[\begin{array}[]{c}{\bar{j}}_{1}^{\ast}(\beta,k_{\perp})\\
{\bar{j}}_{2}^{\ast}(\beta,k_{\perp})\end{array}\Big],

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\star}~e^{-i\beta x_{\star}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\bigg[\begin{array}[]{c}\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{i}(x)\\
\breve{B}_{i}(x)\not{p}_{1}\end{array}\Big]\psi(0)\rangle_{B}~=~{k_{i}\over\beta}\Big[\begin{array}[]{c}j_{1}^{\ast}(\beta,k_{\perp})\\
j_{2}^{\ast}(\beta,k_{\perp})\end{array}\Big]

We need also parametrization of matrix elements with extra
\gamma_{5}
. Since
\breve{A}_{i}\gamma_{5}=i\epsilon_{ij}\breve{A}^{j}
and

\breve{B}_{i}\gamma_{5}=i\epsilon_{ij}\breve{B}^{j}
we get from Eqs. (
9.4
), (
9.4
)

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x)\bigg[\begin{array}[]{c}\breve{A}_{i}(0)\not{p}_{2}\\
\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}(0)\end{array}\bigg]{\gamma_{5}\over\alpha}\psi(x_{\bullet},x_{\perp})\rangle_{A}~=~i\epsilon_{ij}{k^{j}\over\alpha}\Big[\begin{array}[]{c}-j_{1}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\\
j_{2}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\end{array}\Big],

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\bigg[\begin{array}[]{c}\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}(0)\\
\breve{A}_{i}(0)\not{p}_{2}\end{array}\bigg]\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}~=~i\epsilon_{ij}{k^{j}\over\alpha}\Big[\begin{array}[]{c}-{\bar{j}}_{1}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\\
{\bar{j}}_{2}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\end{array}\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)\bigg[\begin{array}[]{c}\breve{A}_{i}(x)\not{p}_{2}\\
\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}(x)\end{array}\bigg]{\gamma_{5}\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}~=~i\epsilon_{ij}{k^{j}\over\alpha}\Big[\begin{array}[]{c}{\bar{j}}_{1}^{\ast}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\\
-{\bar{j}}_{2}^{\ast}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\end{array}\Big],

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(0)\bigg[\begin{array}[]{c}\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}(x)\\
\breve{A}_{i}(x)\not{p}_{2}\end{array}\bigg]\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}~=~i\epsilon_{ij}{k^{j}\over\alpha}\Big[\begin{array}[]{c}j_{1}^{\ast}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\\
-j_{2}^{\ast}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\end{array}\Big],

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x)\bigg[\begin{array}[]{c}\breve{A}_{i}(0)\not{p}_{2}\\
\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}(0)\end{array}\bigg]{\gamma_{5}\over\alpha}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}~=~i\epsilon_{ij}{k^{j}\over\alpha}\Big[\begin{array}[]{c}-j_{1}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\\
j_{2}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\end{array}\Big],

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(x)\bigg[\begin{array}[]{c}\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}(0)\\
\breve{A}_{i}(0)\not{p}_{2}\end{array}\bigg]\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\rangle_{A}~=~i\epsilon_{ij}{k^{j}\over\alpha}\Big[\begin{array}[]{c}-{\bar{j}}_{1}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\\
{\bar{j}}_{2}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\end{array}\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)\bigg[\begin{array}[]{c}\breve{A}_{i}(x)\not{p}_{2}\\
\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}(x)\end{array}\bigg]{\gamma_{5}\over\alpha}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}~=~i\epsilon_{ij}{k^{j}\over\alpha}\Big[\begin{array}[]{c}{\bar{j}}_{1}^{\ast}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\\
-{\bar{j}}_{2}^{\ast}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\end{array}\Big],

\displaystyle\hskip-8.53581pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\bullet}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\bullet}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\alpha}\big)(0)\bigg[\begin{array}[]{c}\not{p}_{2}\breve{A}_{i}(x)\\
\breve{A}_{i}(x)\not{p}_{2}\end{array}\bigg]\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\rangle_{A}~=~i\epsilon_{ij}{k^{j}\over\alpha}\Big[\begin{array}[]{c}j_{1}^{\ast}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\\
-j_{2}^{\ast}(\alpha,k_{\perp})\end{array}\Big]

where
x\equiv x_{\bullet},x_{\ast}
,
and

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\star}~e^{-i\beta x_{\star}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(x)\bigg[\begin{array}[]{c}\breve{B}_{i}(0)\not{p}_{1}\\
\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{i}(0)\end{array}\bigg]{\gamma_{5}\over\beta}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}~=~i\epsilon_{ij}{k^{j}\over\beta}\Big[\begin{array}[]{c}-j_{1}(\beta,k_{\perp})\\
j_{2}(\beta,k_{\perp})\end{array}\Big],

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\star}~e^{-i\alpha x_{\star}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\beta}\big)(x)\bigg[\begin{array}[]{c}\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{i}(0)\\
\breve{B}_{i}(0)\not{p}_{1}\end{array}\bigg]\gamma_{5}\psi(x)\rangle_{B}~=~i\epsilon_{ij}{k^{j}\over\beta}\Big[\begin{array}[]{c}-{\bar{j}}_{1}(\beta,k_{\perp})\\
{\bar{j}}_{2}(\beta,k_{\perp})\end{array}\Big],

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\star}~e^{-i\beta x_{\star}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle{\bar{\psi}}(0)\bigg[\begin{array}[]{c}\breve{B}_{i}(x)\not{p}_{1}\\
\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{i}(x)\end{array}\bigg]{\gamma_{5}\over\beta}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}~=~i\epsilon_{ij}{k^{j}\over\beta}\Big[\begin{array}[]{c}{\bar{j}}_{1}^{\ast}(\beta,k_{\perp})\\
-{\bar{j}}_{2}^{\ast}(\beta,k_{\perp})\end{array}\Big],

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over 8\pi^{3}s}\!\int\!d^{2}x_{\perp}dx_{\star}~e^{-i\beta x_{\star}+i(k,x)_{\perp}}\langle\big({\bar{\psi}}{1\over\beta}\big)(0)\bigg[\begin{array}[]{c}\not{p}_{1}\breve{B}_{i}(x)\\
\breve{B}_{i}(x)\not{p}_{1}\end{array}\bigg]\gamma_{5}\psi(0)\rangle_{B}~=~i\epsilon_{ij}{k^{j}\over\beta}\Big[\begin{array}[]{c}j_{1}^{\ast}(\beta,k_{\perp})\\
-j_{2}^{\ast}(\beta,k_{\perp})\end{array}\Big]

where
x\equiv x_{\star},x_{\ast}

It is convenient to introduce the following combinations for the parametrization of the exchange-type power corrections

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptJ^{1ff^{\prime}}_{++}(q,k_{\perp})~=~(j_{1}+{\bar{j}}_{1})^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\ast}_{1}+{\bar{j}}^{\ast}_{1})^{f^{\prime}}(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})~+~{\rm c.c.},

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptJ^{2ff^{\prime}}_{++}(q,k_{\perp})~=~(j_{2}+{\bar{j}}_{2})^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\ast}_{2}+{\bar{j}}^{\ast}_{2})^{f^{\prime}}(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})~+~{\rm c.c.},

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptJ^{1ff^{\prime}}_{--}(q,k_{\perp})~=~(j_{1}-{\bar{j}}_{1})^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\ast}_{1}-{\bar{j}}^{\ast}_{1})^{f^{\prime}}(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})~+~{\rm c.c.},

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptJ^{2ff^{\prime}}_{--}(q,k_{\perp})~=~(j_{2}-{\bar{j}}_{2})^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\ast}_{2}-{\bar{j}}^{\ast}_{2})^{f^{\prime}}(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})~+~{\rm c.c.},

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptJ^{1ff^{\prime}}_{+-}(q,k_{\perp})~=~(j_{1}+{\bar{j}}_{1})^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\ast}_{1}-{\bar{j}}^{\ast}_{1})^{f^{\prime}}(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})~+~{\rm c.c.},

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptJ^{1ff^{\prime}}_{-+}(q,k_{\perp})~=~(j_{1}-{\bar{j}}_{1})^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\ast}_{1}+{\bar{j}}^{\ast}_{1})^{f^{\prime}}(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})~+~{\rm c.c.},

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptJ^{2ff^{\prime}}_{+-}(q,k_{\perp})~=~(j_{2}+{\bar{j}}_{2})^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\ast}_{2}-{\bar{j}}^{\ast}_{2})^{f^{\prime}}(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})~+~{\rm c.c.},

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptJ^{2ff^{\prime}}_{-+}(q,k_{\perp})~=~(j_{2}-{\bar{j}}_{2})^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\ast}_{2}+{\bar{j}}^{\ast}_{2})^{f^{\prime}}(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})~+~{\rm c.c.},

(450)

and

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptI^{1ff^{\prime}}_{--}(q,k_{\perp})~=~i(j_{1}-{\bar{j}}_{1})^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\ast}_{1}-{\bar{j}}^{\ast}_{1})^{f^{\prime}}(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})~+~{\rm c.c.},

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptI^{2ff^{\prime}}_{--}(q,k_{\perp})~=~i(j_{2}-{\bar{j}}_{2})^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\ast}_{2}-{\bar{j}}^{\ast}_{2})^{f^{\prime}}(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})~+~{\rm c.c.},

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptI^{1ff^{\prime}}_{+-}(q,k_{\perp})~=~i(j_{1}+{\bar{j}}_{1})^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\ast}_{1}-{\bar{j}}^{\ast}_{1})^{f^{\prime}}(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})~+~{\rm c.c.},

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptI^{1ff^{\prime}}_{-+}(q,k_{\perp})~=~i(j_{1}-{\bar{j}}_{1})^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\ast}_{1}+{\bar{j}}^{\ast}_{1})^{f^{\prime}}(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})~+~{\rm c.c.},

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptI^{2ff^{\prime}}_{+-}(q,k_{\perp})~=~i(j_{2}+{\bar{j}}_{2})^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\ast}_{2}-{\bar{j}}^{\ast}_{2})^{f^{\prime}}(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})~+~{\rm c.c.}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptI^{2ff^{\prime}}_{-+}(q,k_{\perp})~=~i(j_{2}-{\bar{j}}_{2})^{f}(\alpha_{q},k_{\perp})(j^{\ast}_{2}+{\bar{j}}^{\ast}_{2})^{f^{\prime}}(\beta_{q},(q-k)_{\perp})~+~{\rm c.c.}

(451)

9.5
Products of leptonic tensor and hadronic tensors’ structures

The lepton momenta in the Collins-Soper frame are parametrized as

\hskip 0.0ptl~=~{Q\over 2}(1,\sin\theta\cos\phi,\sin\theta\sin\phi,\cos\theta),~~~l^{\prime}~=~{Q\over 2}(1,-\sin\theta\cos\phi,-\sin\theta\sin\phi,-\cos\theta)

(452)

The products of the symmetric leptonic tensor with unit vectors
X
,
Y
, and
Z
are

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptL^{\mu\nu}\big({q_{\mu}q_{\nu}\over q^{2}}-g_{\mu\nu}\big)~=~2{(q\cdot l)(q\cdot l^{\prime})\over q^{2}}+l\cdot l^{\prime}~=~Q^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptL^{\mu\nu}X_{\mu}X_{\nu}~=~2(l\cdot X)(l^{\prime}\cdot X)+l\cdot l^{\prime}~=~{Q^{2}\over 2}\big[-\sin^{2}\theta\cos^{2}\phi+1\big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptL^{\mu\nu}Z_{\mu}Z_{\nu}~=~2(l\cdot Z)(l^{\prime}\cdot Z)+l\cdot l^{\prime}~=~{Q^{2}\over 2}\big[-\cos^{2}\theta+1\big]~=~{Q^{2}\over 2}\sin^{2}\theta

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptL^{\mu\nu}X_{\mu}Z_{\nu}~=~(l\cdot X)(l^{\prime}\cdot Z)+(l^{\prime}\cdot X)(l\cdot Z)~=~-{Q^{2}\over 4}\sin 2\theta\cos\phi

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptL^{\mu\nu}Y_{\mu}Z_{\nu}~=~(l\cdot Y)(l^{\prime}\cdot Z)+(l^{\prime}\cdot Y)(l\cdot Z)~=~-{Q^{2}\over 4}\sin 2\theta\sin\phi

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526ptL^{\mu\nu}Y_{\mu}X_{\nu}~=~(l\cdot Y)(l^{\prime}\cdot X)+(l^{\prime}\cdot Y)(l\cdot X)~=~-{Q^{2}\over 4}\sin^{2}\theta\sin 2\phi

(453)

and therefore

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt{1\over Q^{2}}L^{\mu\nu}\Big(-\big(g_{\mu\nu}-{q_{\mu}q_{\nu}\over q^{2}}\big)(W_{T}+W_{{\Delta\Delta}})-2X_{\mu}X_{\nu}W_{\Delta\Delta}

(454)

\displaystyle\hskip 39.83385pt+~Z_{\mu}Z_{\nu}(W_{L}-W_{T}-W_{\Delta\Delta})-(X_{\mu}Z_{\nu}+X_{\nu}Z_{\mu})W_{\Delta}\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~{1\over 2}(W_{T}+W_{L})\bigg[1+\cos^{2}\theta{W_{T}-W_{L}\over W_{T}+W_{L}}+\sin 2\theta\sin\phi{W_{\Delta}\over W_{T}+W_{L}}+\sin^{2}\theta\cos 2\phi{W_{\Delta\Delta}\over W_{T}+W_{L}}\bigg]

We also need products of antisymmetric leptonic tensor with hadron sctructures

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt\epsilon_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}l^{\alpha}{l^{\prime}}^{\beta}\epsilon^{\mu\nu\lambda\rho}q_{\lambda}Z_{\rho}~

(455)

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt=~-2[(l\cdot q)(l^{\prime}\cdot Z)-(l^{\prime}\cdot q)(l\cdot Z)]~=~Q[(l\cdot Z)-(l^{\prime}\cdot Z)]~=~-Q^{2}\cos\theta,

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt\epsilon_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}l^{\alpha}{l^{\prime}}^{\beta}\epsilon^{\mu\nu\lambda\rho}q_{\lambda}X_{\rho}~

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt=~-2[(l\cdot q)(l^{\prime}\cdot X)-(l^{\prime}\cdot q)(l\cdot X)]~=~Q[(l\cdot X)-(l^{\prime}\cdot X)]~=~-Q^{2}\sin\theta\cos\phi

and therefore (
Q_{\parallel}=\sqrt{Q^{2}+Q_{\perp}^{2}}
)

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt\epsilon^{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}l_{\alpha}{l^{\prime}}_{\beta}\epsilon_{\mu\nu\lambda\rho}q^{\lambda}\Big[{Z^{\rho}\over Q_{\parallel}}\mathpzc{W}_{1}^{{\rm F}f}(q)+X^{\rho}{Q_{\perp}\over Q_{\parallel}Q}\mathpzc{W}_{2}^{{\rm F}f}(q)\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip-2.84526pt=~-Q^{2}\Big[\mathpzc{W}_{1}^{{\rm F}f}(q)\cos\theta+{Q_{\perp}\over Q}\mathpzc{W}_{2}^{{\rm F}f}(q)\sin\theta\cos\phi\Big]\Big[1+O\big({Q_{\perp}^{2}\over Q^{2}}\big)\Big].

(456)
